                self.exchange.fetch_balance, 
                params={"accountType": V5_UNIFIED_ACCOUNT_TYPE, "coin": settle_currency}, # V5 specific params
                max_retries=self.config.max_fetch_retries, 
                delay_seconds=self.config.retry_delay_seconds
            )
            total_equity, available_balance = Decimal("NaN"), Decimal("NaN")
            
            # Bybit V5 Unified Account structure parsing
            if "info" in balance_data and "result" in balance_data["info"] and "list" in balance_data["info"]["result"]:
                account_list = balance_data["info"]["result"]["list"]
                if account_list and isinstance(account_list, list):
                    # Find the UNIFIED account details
                    unified_acc_info = next((item for item in account_list if item.get("accountType") == V5_UNIFIED_ACCOUNT_TYPE), None)
                    if unified_acc_info:
                        total_equity = safe_decimal(unified_acc_info.get("totalEquity"))
                        # Find specific coin details within the unified account
                        coin_details_list = unified_acc_info.get("coin", [])
                        if coin_details_list and isinstance(coin_details_list, list):
                            settle_coin_info = next((c for c in coin_details_list if c.get("coin") == settle_currency), None)
                            if settle_coin_info:
                                available_balance = safe_decimal(settle_coin_info.get("availableToWithdraw")) # Or "walletBalance" / "availableBalance" depending on needs
                                # If totalEquity was NaN from account level, try coin-specific equity
                                if total_equity.is_nan() and settle_coin_info.get("equity") is not None: 
                                    total_equity = safe_decimal(settle_coin_info.get("equity"))
                        # Fallback for available balance if not found per coin
                        if available_balance.is_nan() and unified_acc_info.get("totalAvailableBalance") is not None:
                            available_balance = safe_decimal(unified_acc_info.get("totalAvailableBalance"))
                            logger.debug(f"Used 'totalAvailableBalance' for {settle_currency} as coin-specific 'availableToWithdraw' was not found/parsed.")
            
            # Fallbacks to CCXT standardized fields if V5 parsing failed or was incomplete
            if total_equity.is_nan() and balance_data.get("total", {}).get(settle_currency) is not None:
                total_equity = safe_decimal(balance_data["total"][settle_currency])
                logger.debug("Used CCXT standardized 'total' balance field as fallback for equity.")
            if available_balance.is_nan() and balance_data.get("free", {}).get(settle_currency) is not None:
                available_balance = safe_decimal(balance_data["free"][settle_currency])
                logger.debug("Used CCXT standardized 'free' balance field as fallback for available.")

            if total_equity.is_nan(): 
                logger.error(f"Could not extract valid total equity for {settle_currency}. Raw 'info.result.list[0]' (truncated): {str(balance_data.get('info',{}).get('result',{}).get('list',[{}])[0])[:300]}");
                return None, available_balance if not available_balance.is_nan() else Decimal("0") # Return available if found, else 0
            if available_balance.is_nan(): 
                logger.warning(f"Could not extract valid available balance for {settle_currency}. Defaulting to 0."); 
                available_balance = Decimal("0")
            
            logger.debug(f"Balance ({settle_currency}): Total Equity = {total_equity.normalize()}, Available = {available_balance.normalize()}")
            return total_equity, available_balance
        except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Failed to fetch or parse balance: {e}", exc_info=True); return None, None

    def get_current_position(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.error("Exchange or market info not available for position fetch."); return None
        market_id = self.market_info.get("id")
        if not market_id: logger.error("Market ID not found in market info. Cannot fetch position."); return None
        
        positions_summary: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {"long": {}, "short": {}} # Initialize for no position
        
        logger.debug(f"Fetching position for {self.config.symbol} (API ID: {market_id}, Cat: {self.config.bybit_v5_category}, PosIdx: {self.config.position_idx})...")
        try:
            fetched_positions_list_ccxt = fetch_with_retries(
                self.exchange.fetch_positions, 
                symbols=[self.config.symbol], # Bybit V5 API can filter by symbol with fetch_positions
                params={"category": self.config.bybit_v5_category, "symbol": market_id}, # V5 specific
                max_retries=self.config.max_fetch_retries, 
                delay_seconds=self.config.retry_delay_seconds
            )
            if not fetched_positions_list_ccxt: 
                logger.debug("No position data returned from fetch_positions (empty list). Assuming flat."); return positions_summary

            target_pos_info_raw_api = None
            # Iterate through unified CCXT position objects to find the one matching our positionIdx
            for pos_data_ccxt_unified in fetched_positions_list_ccxt:
                raw_api_entry = pos_data_ccxt_unified.get("info", {}) # This is the direct API response part
                pos_idx_from_api_str = raw_api_entry.get("positionIdx") # positionIdx is a string from API
                try: 
                    pos_idx_from_api_int = int(pos_idx_from_api_str) if pos_idx_from_api_str is not None else -1 # Default to non-matching
                except ValueError: 
                    logger.warning(f"Could not parse positionIdx '{pos_idx_from_api_str}' from API response. Skipping entry: {raw_api_entry}"); continue
                
                if pos_idx_from_api_int == self.config.position_idx:
                    target_pos_info_raw_api = raw_api_entry
                    logger.debug(f"Found position entry for symbol {market_id} matching configured positionIdx={self.config.position_idx}: {target_pos_info_raw_api}")
                    break # Found the target position
            
            if not target_pos_info_raw_api:
                logger.debug(f"No position entry found for symbol {market_id} with positionIdx={self.config.position_idx}. Assuming flat."); return positions_summary
            
            qty_abs = safe_decimal(target_pos_info_raw_api.get("size", "0")).copy_abs()
            if qty_abs < POSITION_QTY_EPSILON: # Position size is negligible or zero
                logger.debug(f"Position size {qty_abs.normalize()} for Idx {self.config.position_idx} is negligible. Considered flat."); return positions_summary
            
            api_side_str = target_pos_info_raw_api.get("side", "None").lower() # 'Buy', 'Sell', or 'None'
            position_side_key: Optional[str] = None

            # Determine logical side based on positionIdx and API 'side'
            if self.config.position_idx == 0: # One-Way Mode
                if api_side_str == "buy": position_side_key = "long"
                elif api_side_str == "sell": position_side_key = "short"
                elif api_side_str == "none" and qty_abs > Decimal("0"): # Should not happen if size > 0
                    logger.warning(f"Inconsistent state for One-Way (Idx 0): API side 'None' but size {qty_abs.normalize()}. Treating as flat for safety."); return positions_summary
            elif self.config.position_idx == 1: # Hedge Mode - Buy Side (Long)
                position_side_key = "long"
                if api_side_str != "buy" and qty_abs > Decimal("0"): # API side should be 'Buy' if size > 0 for Idx 1
                    logger.warning(f"Hedge Buy (Idx 1) has API side '{api_side_str}' (not 'Buy') with size {qty_abs.normalize()}. Assuming long based on Idx.")
            elif self.config.position_idx == 2: # Hedge Mode - Sell Side (Short)
                position_side_key = "short"
                if api_side_str != "sell" and qty_abs > Decimal("0"): # API side should be 'Sell' if size > 0 for Idx 2
                    logger.warning(f"Hedge Sell (Idx 2) has API side '{api_side_str}' (not 'Sell') with size {qty_abs.normalize()}. Assuming short based on Idx.")

            if position_side_key:
                entry_price = safe_decimal(target_pos_info_raw_api.get("avgPrice", "0"))
                # Ensure prices are positive, else treat as not set (None)
                sl_price_api = safe_decimal(target_pos_info_raw_api.get("stopLoss", "0"))
                sl_price_valid = sl_price_api if not sl_price_api.is_nan() and sl_price_api > Decimal("0") else None
                tp_price_api = safe_decimal(target_pos_info_raw_api.get("takeProfit", "0"))
                tp_price_valid = tp_price_api if not tp_price_api.is_nan() and tp_price_api > Decimal("0") else None
                tsl_trigger_price_api = safe_decimal(target_pos_info_raw_api.get("trailingStop", "0")) # This is trailing stop trigger price
                tsl_active = not tsl_trigger_price_api.is_nan() and tsl_trigger_price_api > Decimal("0")

                positions_summary[position_side_key] = {
                    "qty": qty_abs, 
                    "entry_price": entry_price if not entry_price.is_nan() and entry_price > Decimal("0") else Decimal("NaN"), # Use NaN if invalid entry
                    "liq_price": safe_decimal(target_pos_info_raw_api.get("liqPrice", "0")), 
                    "unrealized_pnl": safe_decimal(target_pos_info_raw_api.get("unrealisedPnl", "0")),
                    "api_side": api_side_str, "info": target_pos_info_raw_api, # Store raw info for debugging
                    "stop_loss_price": sl_price_valid,
                    "take_profit_price": tp_price_valid,
                    "is_tsl_active": tsl_active,
                    "tsl_trigger_price": tsl_trigger_price_api if tsl_active else None,
                }
                entry_str = positions_summary[position_side_key]["entry_price"].normalize() if positions_summary[position_side_key]["entry_price"] and not positions_summary[position_side_key]["entry_price"].is_nan() else "N/A"
                logger.debug(f"Identified {position_side_key.upper()} position (Idx {self.config.position_idx}): Qty={qty_abs.normalize()}, EntryPx={entry_str}")
            else: # This case should be rare if logic above is complete
                logger.warning(f"Position size {qty_abs.normalize()} for Idx {self.config.position_idx} but no long/short mapping (api_side: '{api_side_str}'). Assuming flat."); return positions_summary
            
            return positions_summary
        except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Failed to fetch or parse positions for {self.config.symbol}: {e}", exc_info=True); return None

# --- Indicator Calculator Class ---
class IndicatorCalculator:
    def __init__(self, config: TradingConfig): self.config = config
    def calculate_indicators(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[Dict[str, Union[Decimal, bool, int]]]:
        logger.info(f"{Fore.CYAN}# Weaving indicator patterns (Original & VT)...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        if df is None or df.empty: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}No DataFrame for indicator calculation.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
        
        required_ohlc_cols = ["open", "high", "low", "close", "volume"]
        if not all(c in df.columns for c in required_ohlc_cols):
            missing_cols = [c for c in required_ohlc_cols if c not in df.columns]
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}DataFrame missing required columns for indicators: {missing_cols}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
        
        try:
            df_calc = df[required_ohlc_cols].copy() # Work on a copy with only necessary columns
            
            # Convert to float for TA-Lib / pandas_ta compatibility, handling Decimal and potential NaNs
            def safe_to_float(x: Any) -> float:
                if isinstance(x, (float, int)): return float(x)
                if isinstance(x, Decimal): return float('nan') if x.is_nan() else float(x)
                try: # Attempt conversion for strings
                    val_stripped = str(x).strip().lower()
                    return float('nan') if val_stripped in ("nan", "none", "null", "") else float(val_stripped)
                except (ValueError, TypeError): return float('nan') # Includes None or other unconvertible types
            
            for col in required_ohlc_cols:
                if df_calc[col].empty: df_calc[col] = pd.Series(dtype=float); continue # Handle empty series
                df_calc[col] = df_calc[col].apply(safe_to_float).astype(float) # Apply conversion and ensure float type
            
            initial_len = len(df_calc)
            df_calc.dropna(subset=required_ohlc_cols, inplace=True, how='any') # Drop rows with any NaN in essential columns
            if len(df_calc) < initial_len: 
                logger.debug(f"Dropped {initial_len - len(df_calc)} rows with NaN in OHLCV after float conversion for TA.")
            if df_calc.empty: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}DataFrame empty after NaN drop during indicator pre-processing.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None

            max_period_orig = max(self.config.slow_ema_period, self.config.trend_ema_period, 
                                  self.config.stoch_period + self.config.stoch_smooth_k + self.config.stoch_smooth_d, 
                                  self.config.atr_period, self.config.adx_period * 2) # ADX needs more data
            max_period_vt = 0
            if self.config.vt_enable:
                max_period_vt = max(self.config.vt_trend_ema_period, self.config.vt_vwma_period, self.config.vt_volume_spike_lookback)
            
            min_required_data_length = max(max_period_orig, max_period_vt) + 20 # Buffer for stable calculations
            if len(df_calc) < min_required_data_length: 
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Insufficient data ({len(df_calc)} rows) for robust indicators (needs ~{min_required_data_length}).{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None

            close_s, high_s, low_s, volume_s, open_s = df_calc["close"], df_calc["high"], df_calc["low"], df_calc["volume"], df_calc["open"]

            # --- Original Strategy Indicators ---
            fast_ema_s = close_s.ewm(span=self.config.fast_ema_period, adjust=False).mean()
            slow_ema_s = close_s.ewm(span=self.config.slow_ema_period, adjust=False).mean()
            trend_ema_s = close_s.ewm(span=self.config.trend_ema_period, adjust=False).mean()
            
            low_min_stoch = low_s.rolling(window=self.config.stoch_period, min_periods=max(1, self.config.stoch_period // 2)).min()
            high_max_stoch = high_s.rolling(window=self.config.stoch_period, min_periods=max(1, self.config.stoch_period // 2)).max()
            stoch_range = high_max_stoch - low_min_stoch
            # Avoid division by zero for stoch_k; default to 50 if range is zero
            stoch_k_raw_values = np.where(stoch_range > 1e-12, 100 * (close_s - low_min_stoch) / stoch_range, 50.0)
            stoch_k_raw_s = pd.Series(stoch_k_raw_values, index=df_calc.index).fillna(50) # Fill any initial NaNs
            stoch_k_s = stoch_k_raw_s.rolling(window=self.config.stoch_smooth_k, min_periods=1).mean().fillna(50)
            stoch_d_s = stoch_k_s.rolling(window=self.config.stoch_smooth_d, min_periods=1).mean().fillna(50)
            
            true_range_s = pd.concat([high_s - low_s, (high_s - close_s.shift(1)).abs(), (low_s - close_s.shift(1)).abs()], axis=1).max(axis=1).fillna(0)
            atr_s = true_range_s.ewm(span=self.config.atr_period, adjust=False).mean() # Using EWM for ATR (common)
            adx_s, pdi_s, mdi_s = self._calculate_adx(high_s, low_s, close_s, atr_s, self.config.adx_period)

            # --- VolumaticTrend Strategy Indicators (if enabled) ---
            vt_trend_ema_s_series = pd.Series(np.nan, index=df_calc.index) 
            vt_vwma_s_series = pd.Series(np.nan, index=df_calc.index)
            vt_volume_avg_s_series = pd.Series(np.nan, index=df_calc.index)
            vt_is_volume_spike_s_series = pd.Series(False, index=df_calc.index)

            if self.config.vt_enable:
                vt_trend_ema_s_series = close_s.ewm(span=self.config.vt_trend_ema_period, adjust=False).mean()
                if hasattr(df_calc.ta, 'vwma'): # Use pandas_ta for VWMA
                    vwma_result = df_calc.ta.vwma(length=self.config.vt_vwma_period, fillna=np.nan)
                    if isinstance(vwma_result, pd.Series): vt_vwma_s_series = vwma_result
                    else: logger.warning("pandas_ta.vwma did not return a Series. VWMA will be NaN.")
                else: logger.error("pandas_ta.vwma not found. VWMA calculation skipped (will be NaN).")
                
                vt_volume_avg_s_series = volume_s.rolling(window=self.config.vt_volume_spike_lookback, min_periods=max(1, self.config.vt_volume_spike_lookback // 2)).mean()
                vt_is_volume_spike_s_series = (volume_s > vt_volume_avg_s_series * float(self.config.vt_volume_spike_multiplier)) & (vt_volume_avg_s_series > 0)

            # Helper to get latest valid Decimal value from a Series
            def get_latest_decimal(series: pd.Series, name: str) -> Decimal:
                valid_series = series.dropna()
                if not valid_series.empty: return safe_decimal(str(valid_series.iloc[-1]))
                logger.warning(f"Indicator series '{name}' empty or all NaNs, returning NaN."); return Decimal("NaN")
            # Helper to get latest valid bool value from a Series
            def get_latest_bool(series: pd.Series, name: str) -> bool:
                valid_series = series.dropna()
                if not valid_series.empty: return bool(valid_series.iloc[-1])
                logger.warning(f"Boolean indicator series '{name}' empty or all NaNs, returning False."); return False

            indicators_out: Dict[str, Union[Decimal, bool, int]] = {
                "fast_ema": get_latest_decimal(fast_ema_s, "fast_ema"), "slow_ema": get_latest_decimal(slow_ema_s, "slow_ema"),
                "trend_ema": get_latest_decimal(trend_ema_s, "trend_ema"), "stoch_k": get_latest_decimal(stoch_k_s, "stoch_k"),
                "stoch_d": get_latest_decimal(stoch_d_s, "stoch_d"), "atr": get_latest_decimal(atr_s, "atr"),
                "atr_period": self.config.atr_period, "adx": get_latest_decimal(adx_s, "adx"),
                "pdi": get_latest_decimal(pdi_s, "pdi"), "mdi": get_latest_decimal(mdi_s, "mdi"),
                # VT Indicators (will be NaN/False if VT disabled or calculation failed)
                "vt_trend_ema": get_latest_decimal(vt_trend_ema_s_series, "vt_trend_ema"),
                "vt_vwma": get_latest_decimal(vt_vwma_s_series, "vt_vwma"),
                "vt_volume_avg": get_latest_decimal(vt_volume_avg_s_series, "vt_volume_avg"), # For display/debug
                "vt_is_volume_spike": get_latest_bool(vt_is_volume_spike_s_series, "vt_is_volume_spike"),
                "vt_candle_is_green": (close_s.iloc[-1] > open_s.iloc[-1]) if not close_s.empty and not open_s.empty else False,
                "vt_candle_is_red": (close_s.iloc[-1] < open_s.iloc[-1]) if not close_s.empty and not open_s.empty else False,
            }
            
            # Stochastic K previous and D previous for crossover detection
            stoch_k_valid_series = stoch_k_s.dropna()
            indicators_out["stoch_k_prev"] = get_latest_decimal(stoch_k_valid_series.iloc[:-1] if len(stoch_k_valid_series) >= 2 else pd.Series(dtype=float), "stoch_k_prev")
            stoch_d_valid_series = stoch_d_s.dropna()
            stoch_d_prev_val = get_latest_decimal(stoch_d_valid_series.iloc[:-1] if len(stoch_d_valid_series) >= 2 else pd.Series(dtype=float), "stoch_d_prev")
            
            k_now, d_now, k_prev = indicators_out["stoch_k"], indicators_out["stoch_d"], indicators_out["stoch_k_prev"] # type: ignore
            indicators_out["stoch_kd_bullish"], indicators_out["stoch_kd_bearish"] = False, False
            if not any(v.is_nan() for v in [k_now, d_now, k_prev, stoch_d_prev_val]): # type: ignore
                if (k_prev <= stoch_d_prev_val) and (k_now > d_now): indicators_out["stoch_kd_bullish"] = True # type: ignore
                if (k_prev >= stoch_d_prev_val) and (k_now < d_now): indicators_out["stoch_kd_bearish"] = True # type: ignore

            critical_keys = ["fast_ema", "slow_ema", "trend_ema", "atr", "stoch_k", "stoch_d", "adx", "pdi", "mdi"]
            if self.config.vt_enable: critical_keys.extend(["vt_trend_ema", "vt_vwma"])
            
            failed_indicators = [k for k in critical_keys if not isinstance(indicators_out.get(k), (Decimal, bool, int)) or (isinstance(indicators_out.get(k), Decimal) and indicators_out.get(k, Decimal("NaN")).is_nan())] # type: ignore
            if failed_indicators:
                if indicators_out.get("atr", Decimal("NaN")).is_nan(): # type: ignore
                    logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}CRITICAL: ATR is NaN. Risk calculations will fail. Aborting cycle.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
                logger.warning(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Warning: Critical indicators NaN: {', '.join(failed_indicators)}. May impair signals.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            
            logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Indicator patterns woven successfully.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return indicators_out
        except Exception as e: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Error weaving indicator patterns: {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True); return None

    def _calculate_adx(self, high_s: pd.Series, low_s: pd.Series, close_s: pd.Series, atr_s: pd.Series, period: int) -> Tuple[pd.Series, pd.Series, pd.Series]:
        """Calculates ADX, +DI, -DI. Uses EMA for smoothing (RMA style)."""
        if period <= 0: 
            logger.error("ADX period must be positive."); nan_s = pd.Series(np.nan, index=high_s.index); return nan_s, nan_s, nan_s
        if atr_s.empty or atr_s.isnull().all(): 
            logger.error("ATR series empty/all NaN for ADX calc."); nan_s = pd.Series(np.nan, index=high_s.index); return nan_s, nan_s, nan_s

        move_up, move_down = high_s.diff(), -low_s.diff() # Current high - prev high, prev low - current low
        
        plus_dm_values = np.where((move_up > move_down) & (move_up > 0), move_up, 0.0)
        minus_dm_values = np.where((move_down > move_up) & (move_down > 0), move_down, 0.0)
        
        plus_dm_s = pd.Series(plus_dm_values, index=high_s.index).fillna(0)
        minus_dm_s = pd.Series(minus_dm_values, index=high_s.index).fillna(0)
        
        # Smoothed DM using EMA (adjust=False for RMA-like behavior often used in ADX)
        alpha = 1.0 / period # For EMA smoothing span equivalent
        smoothed_plus_dm_s = plus_dm_s.ewm(alpha=alpha, adjust=False, min_periods=period).mean().fillna(0)
        smoothed_minus_dm_s = minus_dm_s.ewm(alpha=alpha, adjust=False, min_periods=period).mean().fillna(0)
        
        # Calculate +DI and -DI, ensure ATR is not zero or NaN to avoid division errors
        pdi_values = np.where((atr_s > 1e-12) & (~atr_s.isnull()), 100 * smoothed_plus_dm_s / atr_s, 0.0)
        mdi_values = np.where((atr_s > 1e-12) & (~atr_s.isnull()), 100 * smoothed_minus_dm_s / atr_s, 0.0)
        pdi_s_out = pd.Series(pdi_values, index=high_s.index).fillna(0)
        mdi_s_out = pd.Series(mdi_values, index=high_s.index).fillna(0)
        
        di_sum = pdi_s_out + mdi_s_out
        dx_values = np.where(di_sum > 1e-12, 100 * (pdi_s_out - mdi_s_out).abs() / di_sum, 0.0)
        dx_s = pd.Series(dx_values, index=high_s.index).fillna(0)
        
        adx_s_out = dx_s.ewm(alpha=alpha, adjust=False, min_periods=period).mean().fillna(0)
        return adx_s_out, pdi_s_out, mdi_s_out

# --- Signal Generator Class ---
class SignalGenerator:
    def __init__(self, config: TradingConfig): self.config = config

    def _generate_original_signals(self, df_last_candles: pd.DataFrame, indicators: Dict[str, Union[Decimal, bool, int]], current_price: Decimal) -> Tuple[bool, bool, str]:
        """Calculates signals based on the original Pyrmethus strategy logic. Returns (long_signal, short_signal, reason_string_without_prefix)."""
        orig_long_sig, orig_short_sig = False, False
        reason = "Initializing" # Default reason
        
        # Ensure at least 2 candles for prev_close; df_last_candles comes from ohlcv_df.iloc[-N:]
        prev_close = safe_decimal(df_last_candles.iloc[-2]["close"]) if len(df_last_candles) >= 2 else Decimal("NaN")
            
        required_keys = ["stoch_k", "fast_ema", "slow_ema", "trend_ema", "atr", "adx", "pdi", "mdi"]
        ind_values: Dict[str, Decimal] = {}
        # Check for missing or NaN Decimal indicators
        nan_keys = [k for k in required_keys if not isinstance(indicators.get(k), Decimal) or indicators.get(k, Decimal("NaN")).is_nan()] # type: ignore[union-attr]
        if nan_keys:
            return False, False, f"Indicators NaN/Missing: {', '.join(nan_keys)}."
        
        for key in required_keys: ind_values[key] = indicators[key] # type: ignore[assignment]
        k, fast_ema, slow_ema, trend_ema, atr, adx, pdi, mdi = (ind_values[key] for key in required_keys)
        # Boolean indicators
        stoch_kd_bull_cross, stoch_kd_bear_cross = bool(indicators.get("stoch_kd_bullish", False)), bool(indicators.get("stoch_kd_bearish", False))

        ema_bullish_cross, ema_bearish_cross = fast_ema > slow_ema, fast_ema < slow_ema
        ema_cross_state = "Bullish" if ema_bullish_cross else "Bearish" if ema_bearish_cross else "Neutral"
        
        trend_buffer_abs = trend_ema.copy_abs() * (self.config.trend_filter_buffer_percent / Decimal(100))
        price_above_trend_ema = current_price > (trend_ema - trend_buffer_abs)
        price_below_trend_ema = current_price < (trend_ema + trend_buffer_abs)
        trend_allows_long = price_above_trend_ema if self.config.trade_only_with_trend else True
        trend_allows_short = price_below_trend_ema if self.config.trade_only_with_trend else True
        trend_suffix = f"(P:{current_price:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f} vs TrEMA:{trend_ema:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}±{trend_buffer_abs:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f})" if self.config.trade_only_with_trend else "(TrendFilter OFF)"

        stoch_long_cond = (k < self.config.stoch_oversold_threshold) or stoch_kd_bull_cross
        stoch_short_cond = (k > self.config.stoch_overbought_threshold) or stoch_kd_bear_cross
        stoch_reason = f"K:{k:.1f}(OS:{self.config.stoch_oversold_threshold.normalize()}/OB:{self.config.stoch_overbought_threshold.normalize()}) KD_X(B:{stoch_kd_bull_cross}/S:{stoch_kd_bear_cross})"

        significant_move, atr_suffix = True, "(ATRMove OFF)"
        if self.config.atr_move_filter_multiplier > Decimal("0"):
            if atr.is_nan() or atr <= Decimal("0"): atr_suffix, significant_move = f"(ATR Invalid:{atr.normalize() if not atr.is_nan() else 'NaN'})", False
            elif prev_close.is_nan() or prev_close <= Decimal("0"): atr_suffix, significant_move = f"(PrevClose Invalid:{prev_close.normalize() if not prev_close.is_nan() else 'NaN'})", False
            else:
                atr_move_thr = atr * self.config.atr_move_filter_multiplier
                price_move = (current_price - prev_close).copy_abs()
                significant_move = price_move > atr_move_thr
                atr_suffix = f"(Move:{price_move:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f} {'OK' if significant_move else 'LOW'}/{atr_move_thr:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f})"
        
        adx_strong = adx > self.config.min_adx_level
        adx_long_favored, adx_short_favored = pdi > mdi, mdi > pdi
        adx_allows_long, adx_allows_short = adx_strong and adx_long_favored, adx_strong and adx_short_favored
        adx_suffix = f"(ADX:{adx:.1f} {'STR' if adx_strong else 'WEAK'}/{self.config.min_adx_level.normalize()} |Dir:{'P>M' if adx_long_favored else 'M>P' if adx_short_favored else 'N'})"

        base_long, base_short = ema_bullish_cross and stoch_long_cond, ema_bearish_cross and stoch_short_cond
        orig_long_sig = base_long and trend_allows_long and significant_move and adx_allows_long
        orig_short_sig = base_short and trend_allows_short and significant_move and adx_allows_short

        if orig_long_sig: reason = f"Long: EMA_X {ema_cross_state} & StochOK {stoch_reason} & TrendOK {trend_suffix} & ATRMoveOK {atr_suffix} & ADX_OK {adx_suffix}"
        elif orig_short_sig: reason = f"Short: EMA_X {ema_cross_state} & StochOK {stoch_reason} & TrendOK {trend_suffix} & ATRMoveOK {atr_suffix} & ADX_OK {adx_suffix}"
        else: # No signal, construct detailed reason
            parts = [f"NoSignal: Base(EMA_X:{ema_cross_state},Stoch:{stoch_reason}) -> LBase:{base_long}, SBase:{base_short}."]
            if base_long and not trend_allows_long: parts.append(f"LBlk:TrendFail {trend_suffix}.")
            if base_short and not trend_allows_short: parts.append(f"SBlk:TrendFail {trend_suffix}.")
            if (base_long or base_short) and not significant_move: parts.append(f"Blk:ATRMoveFail {atr_suffix}.")
            if base_long and not adx_allows_long: parts.append(f"LBlk:ADXFail {adx_suffix}.")
            if base_short and not adx_allows_short: parts.append(f"SBlk:ADXFail {adx_suffix}.")
            reason = " ".join(parts)
        return orig_long_sig, orig_short_sig, reason

    def _generate_vt_signals(self, indicators: Dict[str, Union[Decimal, bool, int]], current_price: Decimal) -> Tuple[bool, bool, str]:
        """Calculates signals based on VolumaticTrend. Returns (long_signal, short_signal, reason_string_without_prefix)."""
        if not self.config.vt_enable: return False, False, "Disabled by config"
        
        reason = "Initializing"
        required_vt_keys = ["vt_trend_ema", "vt_vwma"] # Decimal indicators
        bool_vt_keys = ["vt_is_volume_spike", "vt_candle_is_green", "vt_candle_is_red"] # Boolean indicators

        ind_values_vt: Dict[str, Decimal] = {}
        nan_decimal_keys = [k for k in required_vt_keys if not isinstance(indicators.get(k), Decimal) or indicators.get(k, Decimal("NaN")).is_nan()] # type: ignore[union-attr]
        invalid_bool_keys = [k for k in bool_vt_keys if not isinstance(indicators.get(k), bool)]
        
        if nan_decimal_keys or invalid_bool_keys:
            missing_details = []
            if nan_decimal_keys: missing_details.append(f"NaN/MissingDecimals: {', '.join(nan_decimal_keys)}")
            if invalid_bool_keys: missing_details.append(f"InvalidBoolType: {', '.join(invalid_bool_keys)}")
            return False, False, f"Indicators Invalid ({'; '.join(missing_details)})."

        for key_vt in required_vt_keys: ind_values_vt[key_vt] = indicators[key_vt] # type: ignore[assignment]
        vt_trend_ema, vt_vwma = ind_values_vt["vt_trend_ema"], ind_values_vt["vt_vwma"]
        is_vol_spike, is_green, is_red = bool(indicators["vt_is_volume_spike"]), bool(indicators["vt_candle_is_green"]), bool(indicators["vt_candle_is_red"])

        price_above_vt_trend = current_price > vt_trend_ema
        price_above_vwma = current_price > vt_vwma
        vt_long_sig = price_above_vt_trend and price_above_vwma and is_vol_spike and is_green
        
        price_below_vt_trend = current_price < vt_trend_ema
        price_below_vwma = current_price < vt_vwma
        vt_short_sig = price_below_vt_trend and price_below_vwma and is_vol_spike and is_red

        if vt_long_sig:
            reason = (f"Long: P>{vt_trend_ema:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}(TrEMA) & P>{vt_vwma:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}(VWMA) & VolSpk & GreenCdl")
        elif vt_short_sig:
            reason = (f"Short: P<{vt_trend_ema:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}(TrEMA) & P<{vt_vwma:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}(VWMA) & VolSpk & RedCdl")
        else: # No VT signal, provide context
            trend_st = "UP" if price_above_vt_trend else "DOWN" if price_below_vt_trend else "NEUTRAL"
            vwma_st = "ABOVE" if price_above_vwma else "BELOW" if price_below_vwma else "NEUTRAL"
            cdl_col = "Green" if is_green else "Red" if is_red else "Neutral"
            reason = (f"NoSignal: Trend(P:{current_price:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f} vs EMA:{vt_trend_ema:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}->{trend_st}), "
                      f"VWMA(P:{current_price:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f} vs VWMA:{vt_vwma:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}->{vwma_st}), "
                      f"VolSpk:{is_vol_spike}, Cdl:{cdl_col}")
        return vt_long_sig, vt_short_sig, reason

    def generate_signals(self, df_last_candles: pd.DataFrame, indicators: Dict[str, Union[Decimal, bool, int]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Generates combined trading signals.
        Returns a dictionary: {"long": bool, "short": bool, "summary": str, "orig_detail": str, "vt_detail": str}
        """
        result: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "long": False, "short": False, 
            "summary": "Initializing signal check", 
            "orig_detail": "N/A", "vt_detail": "N/A"
        }
        if not indicators:
            result.update({"summary": "No Signal: Indicators data missing."}); logger.debug(result["summary"]); return result
        if df_last_candles is None or len(df_last_candles) < 2: 
            reason_no_candle = f"No Signal: Insufficient candle data (needs >=2, got {len(df_last_candles) if df_last_candles is not None else 0})."
            result.update({"summary": reason_no_candle}); logger.debug(reason_no_candle); return result
        
        try:
            current_price = safe_decimal(df_last_candles.iloc[-1]["close"])
            if current_price.is_nan() or current_price <= Decimal("0"):
                reason_invalid_price = f"No Signal: Invalid current price ({current_price.normalize() if not current_price.is_nan() else 'NaN'})."
                result.update({"summary": reason_invalid_price}); logger.warning(reason_invalid_price); return result
            
            orig_long, orig_short, orig_reason = self._generate_original_signals(df_last_candles, indicators, current_price)
            result["orig_detail"] = orig_reason

            vt_long, vt_short, vt_reason = self._generate_vt_signals(indicators, current_price)
            result["vt_detail"] = vt_reason
            
            final_long, final_short = False, False
            summary_status = "No Signal"

            if orig_long and vt_long:
                final_long = True; summary_status = "Orig & VT Long Signal"
            elif orig_short and vt_short:
                final_short = True; summary_status = "Orig & VT Short Signal"
            elif (orig_long and vt_short) or (orig_short and vt_long):
                summary_status = f"Signal Conflict: Orig({'L' if orig_long else 'S' if orig_short else 'N'}) vs VT({'L' if vt_long else 'S' if vt_short else 'N'}). Holding."
            elif orig_long:
                final_long = True; summary_status = "Original Long Signal"
            elif orig_short:
                final_short = True; summary_status = "Original Short Signal"
            elif vt_long:
                final_long = True; summary_status = "VT Long Signal"
            elif vt_short:
                final_short = True; summary_status = "VT Short Signal"
            # Else: No signal from either, summary_status remains "No Signal"
            
            result["long"] = final_long
            result["short"] = final_short
            result["summary"] = summary_status

            log_level = logging.INFO if final_long or final_short or "Conflict" in summary_status or "Block" in orig_reason or "Block" in vt_reason else logging.DEBUG
            logger.log(log_level, f"Signal Check: {summary_status} | Orig: {orig_reason} | VT: {vt_reason}")

        except Exception as e: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Error generating entry signals: {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True)
            result.update({"summary": f"No Signal: Exception ({type(e).__name__})", "long": False, "short": False, "orig_detail": str(e), "vt_detail": "N/A"})
        return result

    def check_exit_signals(self, position_side: str, indicators: Dict[str, Union[Decimal, bool, int]]) -> Optional[str]:
        """Checks for exit signals based on current strategy logic. Returns reason string if exit signaled."""
        if not indicators: logger.warning("Cannot check exit signals: indicators data missing."); return None
        
        # Original Strategy Exit Logic
        fast_ema_val, slow_ema_val = indicators.get("fast_ema"), indicators.get("slow_ema")
        stoch_k_curr, stoch_k_prev = indicators.get("stoch_k"), indicators.get("stoch_k_prev")
        orig_exit_reason: Optional[str] = None
        
        # Check all required Decimal indicators are valid
        orig_req_decs = {"fast_ema": fast_ema_val, "slow_ema": slow_ema_val, "stoch_k": stoch_k_curr, "stoch_k_prev": stoch_k_prev}
        orig_inds_valid = all(isinstance(v, Decimal) and not v.is_nan() for v in orig_req_decs.values())
        
        if orig_inds_valid:
            fast_ema, slow_ema, k_curr, k_prev = fast_ema_val, slow_ema_val, stoch_k_curr, stoch_k_prev # type: ignore
            ema_bull_cross, ema_bear_cross = fast_ema > slow_ema, fast_ema < slow_ema
            os_lvl, ob_lvl = self.config.stoch_oversold_threshold, self.config.stoch_overbought_threshold

            if position_side == "long":
                if ema_bear_cross: orig_exit_reason = f"Orig Exit (L): EMA Bearish Cross (F {fast_ema.normalize()} < S {slow_ema.normalize()})"
                elif k_prev >= ob_lvl and k_curr < ob_lvl: orig_exit_reason = f"Orig Exit (L): Stoch Reversal OB (PrK {k_prev.normalize():.1f}>=OB -> CurK {k_curr.normalize():.1f}<OB)"
            elif position_side == "short":
                if ema_bull_cross: orig_exit_reason = f"Orig Exit (S): EMA Bullish Cross (F {fast_ema.normalize()} > S {slow_ema.normalize()})"
                elif k_prev <= os_lvl and k_curr > os_lvl: orig_exit_reason = f"Orig Exit (S): Stoch Reversal OS (PrK {k_prev.normalize():.1f}<=OS -> CurK {k_curr.normalize():.1f}>OS)"
        
        if orig_exit_reason:
            logger.trade(f"{Fore.YELLOW}{orig_exit_reason}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return orig_exit_reason

        # VolumaticTrend Exit Logic (if enabled and original didn't fire)
        if self.config.vt_enable:
            vt_exit_reason: Optional[str] = None
            # 'close_price' should be added to indicators dict by TradingBot cycle, representing current market price
            price_val, vt_trend_ema_val, vt_vwma_val = indicators.get("close_price"), indicators.get("vt_trend_ema"), indicators.get("vt_vwma")
            is_vol_spike, is_red_cdl, is_green_cdl = indicators.get("vt_is_volume_spike", False), indicators.get("vt_candle_is_red", False), indicators.get("vt_candle_is_green", False)

            vt_req_decs = {"price": price_val, "vt_trend_ema": vt_trend_ema_val, "vt_vwma": vt_vwma_val}
            vt_decs_valid = all(isinstance(v, Decimal) and not v.is_nan() for v in vt_req_decs.values())
            vt_bools_valid = all(isinstance(v, bool) for v in [is_vol_spike, is_red_cdl, is_green_cdl])

            if vt_decs_valid and vt_bools_valid:
                price, vt_trend_ema, vt_vwma = price_val, vt_trend_ema_val, vt_vwma_val # type: ignore
                if position_side == "long":
                    if price < vt_trend_ema: vt_exit_reason = f"VT Exit (L): Price < VT Trend EMA ({vt_trend_ema.normalize()})" # type: ignore
                    elif price < vt_vwma and is_vol_spike and is_red_cdl: vt_exit_reason = f"VT Exit (L): Price < VT VWMA ({vt_vwma.normalize()}) + VolSpike on RedCdl" # type: ignore
                elif position_side == "short":
                    if price > vt_trend_ema: vt_exit_reason = f"VT Exit (S): Price > VT Trend EMA ({vt_trend_ema.normalize()})" # type: ignore
                    elif price > vt_vwma and is_vol_spike and is_green_cdl: vt_exit_reason = f"VT Exit (S): Price > VT VWMA ({vt_vwma.normalize()}) + VolSpike on GreenCdl" # type: ignore
            
            if vt_exit_reason:
                logger.trade(f"{Fore.YELLOW}{vt_exit_reason}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return vt_exit_reason
        
        return None # No exit signal from any strategy

# --- Order Manager Class ---
class OrderManager:
    def __init__(self, config: TradingConfig, exchange_manager: ExchangeManager):
        self.config = config
        self.exchange_manager = exchange_manager
        if not exchange_manager or not exchange_manager.exchange or not exchange_manager.market_info:
            err_msg = "OrderManager init failed: Valid ExchangeManager with initialized exchange and market_info required."
            logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}{err_msg}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); raise ValueError(err_msg)
        self.exchange = exchange_manager.exchange
        self.market_info = exchange_manager.market_info
        # Tracks local understanding of protection status: None, "ACTIVE_SLTP", "ACTIVE_TSL"
        self.protection_tracker: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {"long": None, "short": None}

    def _calculate_trade_parameters(self, side: str, atr: Decimal, total_equity: Decimal, current_price: Decimal) -> Optional[Dict[str, Optional[Decimal]]]:
        if atr.is_nan() or atr <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Invalid ATR ({atr.normalize() if not atr.is_nan() else 'NaN'}) for trade params."); return None
        if total_equity.is_nan() or total_equity <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Invalid equity ({total_equity.normalize() if not total_equity.is_nan() else 'NaN'}) for params."); return None
        if current_price.is_nan() or current_price <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Invalid price ({current_price.normalize() if not current_price.is_nan() else 'NaN'}) for params."); return None
        
        mkt_tick_size = self.market_info.get('tick_size', Decimal('NaN'))
        mkt_contract_size = self.market_info.get('contract_size', Decimal('NaN'))
        mkt_min_order_size = self.market_info.get('min_order_size', Decimal('NaN'))

        if not self.market_info or any(v.is_nan() for v in [mkt_tick_size, mkt_contract_size, mkt_min_order_size]):
             logger.error("Market info (tick_size, contract_size, min_order_size) incomplete for param calculation."); return None
        if side not in ("buy", "sell"): logger.error(f"Invalid side '{side}' for param calc."); return None

        try:
            risk_amt_settle_ccy = total_equity * self.config.risk_percentage
            sl_dist_atr_pts = atr * self.config.sl_atr_multiplier
            sl_price_calc = current_price - sl_dist_atr_pts if side == "buy" else current_price + sl_dist_atr_pts
            if sl_price_calc <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Calculated SL price ({sl_price_calc:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}) invalid (<=0)."); return None

            sl_dist_abs = (current_price - sl_price_calc).copy_abs()
            if mkt_tick_size <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Market tick_size invalid/zero."); return None
            if sl_dist_abs < mkt_tick_size: 
                logger.warning(f"SL distance ({sl_dist_abs.normalize()}) < tick size ({mkt_tick_size.normalize()}). Adjusting to tick size."); sl_dist_abs = mkt_tick_size
                sl_price_calc = current_price - sl_dist_abs if side == "buy" else current_price + sl_dist_abs
                if sl_price_calc <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Adjusted SL price ({sl_price_calc:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}) still invalid."); return None
            if sl_dist_abs <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"SL distance ({sl_dist_abs.normalize()}) invalid (<=0)."); return None

            qty_calc_base: Decimal
            if self.config.market_type == "inverse": # Risk in base, SL distance in quote
                if current_price <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Invalid current_price for inverse qty calc."); return None
                # For inverse, risk_amt_settle_ccy is in BASE ccy. sl_dist_abs is in QUOTE ccy.
                # Qty (contracts) = (Risk Amount in Base * Entry Price) / SL Distance in Quote
                # This assumes 1 contract = 1 USD. If contract value is different (e.g. 100 USD), contract_size needs to be used.
                # Bybit inverse contracts are typically valued at 1 USD of base currency (e.g. 1 contract of BTC/USD is 1 USD worth of BTC)
                # So, qty_calc_base is actually in units of quote currency (e.g. USD for BTC/USD)
                # Qty (USD) = Risk Amount (BTC) * Price (USD/BTC) / SL Distance (USD/BTC) / ContractValuePerPoint (USD)
                # Qty (Contracts) = Risk Amount (BTC) * Price (USD/BTC) / SL Distance (USD)
                # Example: Risk 0.01 BTC, Price 50000 USD/BTC, SL Distance 100 USD. Qty = (0.01 * 50000) / 100 = 5 contracts (5 USD)
                qty_calc_base = (risk_amt_settle_ccy * current_price) / sl_dist_abs
            else: # Linear: Risk in quote, SL distance in quote
                  # Qty (Base Asset) = Risk Amount (Quote) / (SL Distance (Quote) * Contract Size (Base/Contract))
                  # Bybit linear contracts: contractSize is usually 1 (e.g. 1 BTC for BTC/USDT)
                value_change_per_pt_base_unit = mkt_contract_size # Settle ccy change per 1 point price move, per 1 unit of base asset
                if value_change_per_pt_base_unit <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Invalid contract size for linear qty."); return None
                risk_per_unit_base = sl_dist_abs * value_change_per_pt_base_unit
                if risk_per_unit_base <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Zero/negative risk per unit base ({risk_per_unit_base.normalize()})."); return None
                qty_calc_base = risk_amt_settle_ccy / risk_per_unit_base
            
            qty_str_fmt = self.exchange_manager.format_amount(qty_calc_base, ROUND_DOWN)
            qty_final_dec = safe_decimal(qty_str_fmt)
            if qty_final_dec.is_nan() or qty_final_dec <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Calculated qty ({qty_str_fmt}) invalid/zero. Original: {qty_calc_base.normalize()}"); return None
            if qty_final_dec < mkt_min_order_size: logger.error(f"Calculated qty {qty_final_dec.normalize()} < min order size {mkt_min_order_size.normalize()}."); return None

            tp_price_calc: Optional[Decimal] = None
            if self.config.tp_atr_multiplier > Decimal("0"):
                tp_dist_atr_pts = atr * self.config.tp_atr_multiplier
                tp_price_calc = current_price + tp_dist_atr_pts if side == "buy" else current_price - tp_dist_atr_pts
                if tp_price_calc <= Decimal("0"): logger.warning(f"Calculated TP price ({tp_price_calc:.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}) invalid. Disabling TP."); tp_price_calc = None
            
            tsl_dist_pts = current_price * (self.config.trailing_stop_percent / Decimal(100))
            if tsl_dist_pts < mkt_tick_size: logger.debug(f"TSL distance ({tsl_dist_pts.normalize()}) < tick size ({mkt_tick_size.normalize()}). Adjusting."); tsl_dist_pts = mkt_tick_size
            tsl_dist_str_fmt = self.exchange_manager.format_price(tsl_dist_pts) # TSL distance is a price difference
            tsl_dist_final_dec = safe_decimal(tsl_dist_str_fmt)
            if tsl_dist_final_dec.is_nan() or tsl_dist_final_dec <= Decimal("0"):
                logger.warning(f"Invalid TSL distance ('{tsl_dist_str_fmt}'). TSL might fail. Original: {tsl_dist_pts.normalize()}"); tsl_dist_final_dec = Decimal('NaN')

            sl_price_str_fmt = self.exchange_manager.format_price(sl_price_calc)
            sl_price_final_dec = safe_decimal(sl_price_str_fmt)
            if sl_price_final_dec.is_nan() or sl_price_final_dec <= Decimal("0"): logger.error(f"Formatted SL price ('{sl_price_str_fmt}') invalid."); return None
            
            tp_price_final_dec: Optional[Decimal] = None
            if tp_price_calc is not None:
                 tp_price_str_fmt = self.exchange_manager.format_price(tp_price_calc)
                 tp_price_final_dec = safe_decimal(tp_price_str_fmt)
                 if tp_price_final_dec.is_nan() or tp_price_final_dec <= Decimal("0"): logger.warning(f"Failed to format TP price ('{tp_price_str_fmt}'). Disabling TP."); tp_price_final_dec = None

            params: Dict[str, Optional[Decimal]] = {"qty": qty_final_dec, "sl_price": sl_price_final_dec, "tp_price": tp_price_final_dec, "tsl_distance": tsl_dist_final_dec if not tsl_dist_final_dec.is_nan() else None}
            settle_ccy = self.market_info.get('settle', 'SETTLE')
            logger.info(
                f"Trade Params ({side.upper()}): Qty={params['qty'].normalize()} {self.market_info.get('base', 'BASE')}, " # type: ignore
                f"EntryPx≈{current_price.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, SL={params['sl_price'].normalize()}, " # type: ignore
                f"TP={'Disabled' if not params['tp_price'] else params['tp_price'].normalize()}, " # type: ignore
                f"TSL_Dist={'N/A' if not params['tsl_distance'] else params['tsl_distance'].normalize()}, " # type: ignore
                f"RiskAmt≈{risk_amt_settle_ccy.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f} {settle_ccy}, ATR={atr.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP+1}f}"
            )
            return params
        except (InvalidOperation, DivisionByZero, TypeError, Exception) as e: logger.error(f"Error calculating trade params for {side.upper()}: {e}", exc_info=True); return None

    def _execute_market_order(self, side: str, qty_decimal: Decimal) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.error("Market order: Exchange/Market info missing."); return None
        
        qty_str_api = self.exchange_manager.format_amount(qty_decimal, rounding_mode=ROUND_DOWN)
        final_qty_dec_log = safe_decimal(qty_str_api)
        if final_qty_dec_log.is_nan() or final_qty_dec_log <= Decimal("0"):
            logger.error(f"Market order with zero/invalid formatted qty: '{qty_str_api}' (OrigDec: {qty_decimal.normalize()}). Aborted."); return None
        try: amount_float_ccxt = float(qty_str_api)
        except ValueError: logger.error(f"Could not convert qty '{qty_str_api}' to float for API. Aborted."); return None

        logger.trade(f"{Fore.CYAN}Attempting MARKET {side.upper()} order: {final_qty_dec_log.normalize()} {self.market_info.get('base', '')} for {self.config.symbol}...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        try:
            # Bybit V5 market orders for futures are typically IOC.
            # Some exchanges might allow FOK for market, but IOC is safer for partial fills vs outright rejection.
            # For Bybit, market orders are effectively IOC or FOK depending on liquidity.
            # Explicitly setting timeInForce might be needed for some exchanges or if default isn't desired.
            params_v5 = {
                "category": self.config.bybit_v5_category, 
                "positionIdx": self.config.position_idx,
                "timeInForce": "ImmediateOrCancel" # Ensures it doesn't linger if not fillable
            }
            order_resp = fetch_with_retries(
                self.exchange.create_market_order, 
                symbol=self.config.symbol, side=side, amount=amount_float_ccxt, params=params_v5,
                max_retries=self.config.max_fetch_retries, delay_seconds=self.config.retry_delay_seconds
            )
            if order_resp is None: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order submission failed (returned None).{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None

            order_id, status = order_resp.get("id","[N/A]"), order_resp.get("status","[unknown]")
            filled_qty, avg_px = safe_decimal(order_resp.get("filled","0")), safe_decimal(order_resp.get("average","0"))
            avg_px_log = avg_px.normalize() if not avg_px.is_nan() and avg_px > Decimal("0") else "[N/A]"
            
            logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Market order submitted: ID {order_id}, Side {side.upper()}, Ordered {final_qty_dec_log.normalize()}, Status: {status}, Filled: {filled_qty.normalize()}, AvgPx: {avg_px_log}{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Order Submitted", f"Market {side.upper()} {final_qty_dec_log.normalize()} ID:{order_id}, Status:{status}")

            if status == "rejected": 
                reject_reason = order_resp.get('info', {}).get('rejectReason', order_resp.get('info', {}).get('retMsg', 'No reason provided'))
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order {order_id} REJECTED. Reason: '{reject_reason}'. Info: {order_resp.get('info')}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
            if status == "canceled" and filled_qty == Decimal("0") and params_v5.get("timeInForce") == "ImmediateOrCancel":
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order {order_id} (IOC) CANCELED with 0 filled. No execution.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
            if status == "expired": logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order {order_id} EXPIRED. Unexpected for market.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
            
            logger.debug(f"Delay ({self.config.order_check_delay_seconds}s) after market order {order_id} for propagation..."); time.sleep(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds)
            return order_resp
        except (ccxt.InsufficientFunds, ccxt.InvalidOrder) as e: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Order placement failed ({type(e).__name__}): {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=False)
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Order FAILED", f"Market {side.upper()} failed: {str(e)[:50]}"); return None
        except Exception as e: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Unexpected error placing market order: {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True)
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Order ERROR", f"Market {side.upper()} unexpected error."); return None

    def _set_position_protection(self, position_side: str, sl_price: Optional[Decimal] = None, tp_price: Optional[Decimal] = None, is_tsl: bool = False, tsl_distance: Optional[Decimal] = None, tsl_activation_price: Optional[Decimal] = None) -> bool:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.error("Protection: Exchange/Market info missing."); return False
        market_id = self.market_info.get("id")
        if not market_id: logger.error("Protection: Market ID missing."); return False
        
        tracker_key = position_side.lower() # "long" or "short"
        if tracker_key not in self.protection_tracker: logger.error(f"Invalid position_side '{position_side}' for protection tracker."); return False

        # Format parameters for API: "0" means clear/disable that specific stop type
        sl_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(sl_price, "price", allow_zero=True) if sl_price else "0"
        tp_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(tp_price, "price", allow_zero=True) if tp_price else "0"
        tsl_dist_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(tsl_distance, "distance", allow_zero=False) if tsl_distance else "0" # TSL dist cannot be 0 if active
        tsl_act_px_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(tsl_activation_price, "price", allow_zero=False) if tsl_activation_price else "0" # Activation px cannot be 0

        api_params: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "category": self.config.bybit_v5_category, 
            "symbol": market_id, 
            "positionIdx": self.config.position_idx, 
            "tpslMode": V5_TPSL_MODE_FULL # Assumes "Full" TP/SL mode for the entire position
        }
        action_desc, new_tracker_state = "", None

        if is_tsl:
            if tsl_dist_api_str and tsl_dist_api_str != "0" and tsl_act_px_api_str and tsl_act_px_api_str != "0":
                api_params.update({
                    "trailingStop": tsl_dist_api_str, 
                    "activePrice": tsl_act_px_api_str, # Activation price for TSL
                    "triggerBy": self.config.tsl_trigger_by,
                    "stopLoss": "0", "takeProfit": "0" # Clear regular SL/TP when TSL is active
                })
                action_desc, new_tracker_state = f"ACTIVATE/MODIFY TSL (Dist: {tsl_dist_api_str}, ActPx: {tsl_act_px_api_str})", "ACTIVE_TSL"
            else: logger.error(f"Cannot activate TSL for {position_side.upper()}: Invalid TSL distance ('{tsl_dist_api_str}') or act price ('{tsl_act_px_api_str}')."); return False
        elif sl_api_str != "0" or tp_api_str != "0": # Setting SL and/or TP
            if sl_api_str != "0": api_params["stopLoss"] = sl_api_str
            if tp_api_str != "0": api_params["takeProfit"] = tp_api_str
            api_params.update({ # Ensure trigger types are set, and TSL is cleared
                "slTriggerBy": self.config.sl_trigger_by, 
                "tpTriggerBy": self.config.sl_trigger_by, # Bybit uses same trigger for TP as SL by default
                "trailingStop": "0", "activePrice": "0" # Clear TSL if setting SL/TP
            })
            action_desc, new_tracker_state = f"SET SL={api_params.get('stopLoss','0')} TP={api_params.get('takeProfit','0')}", "ACTIVE_SLTP"
        else: # Clearing all protection
            api_params.update({"stopLoss": "0", "takeProfit": "0", "trailingStop": "0", "activePrice": "0"})
            action_desc, new_tracker_state = "CLEAR ALL SL/TP/TSL", None
        
        logger.trade(f"{Fore.CYAN}Attempting to {action_desc} for {position_side.upper()} {self.config.symbol}...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        logger.debug(f"Calling V5 setTradingStop with params: {api_params}")
        
        # This uses a private method name from CCXT; this can be fragile if CCXT changes internals.
        # A comment highlighting this specific dependency is good.
        # TODO: Monitor CCXT for a standardized public method for v5/position/trading-stop.
        private_method_name = "privatePostPositionTradingStop" 
        if not hasattr(self.exchange, private_method_name): 
            logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}Fatal: CCXT method '{private_method_name}' not found. Cannot manage position protection.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return False
        
        method_to_call = getattr(self.exchange, private_method_name)
        try:
            response = fetch_with_retries(method_to_call, params=api_params, max_retries=self.config.max_fetch_retries, delay_seconds=self.config.retry_delay_seconds)
            if response and response.get("retCode") == V5_SUCCESS_RETCODE:
                logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}{action_desc} successful for {position_side.upper()}.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
                termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Protection {('Set' if new_tracker_state else 'Cleared')}", f"{action_desc} for {position_side.upper()}")
                self.protection_tracker[tracker_key] = new_tracker_state; return True
            else:
                ret_code = response.get("retCode", "[N/A]") if response else "[No Resp]"
                ret_msg = response.get("retMsg", "[No Msg]") if response else "[No Resp]"
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}{action_desc} failed for {position_side.upper()}. API: Code={ret_code}, Msg='{ret_msg}'.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); logger.debug(f"Full response from failed {private_method_name}: {response}")
                termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Protection FAILED", f"{action_desc[:30]}... failed: {ret_msg[:50]}"); return False
        except Exception as e: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Unexpected error during '{action_desc}' for {position_side.upper()}: {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True)
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Protection ERROR", f"{action_desc[:30]}... error."); return False

    def _verify_position_state(self, expected_side_logical: Optional[str], expected_qty_min_abs: Decimal = POSITION_QTY_EPSILON, max_attempts: int = 4, delay_seconds: float = 1.5, action_context: str = "Position Verification") -> Tuple[bool, Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]]:
        logger.debug(f"{action_context}: Verifying. Expect side: '{expected_side_logical}', MinQty≈{expected_qty_min_abs.normalize()}. Attempts: {max_attempts}.")
        last_pos_summary: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]] = None
        for attempt in range(max_attempts):
            logger.debug(f"{action_context}: Attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_attempts}...")
            current_pos_summary = self.exchange_manager.get_current_position(); last_pos_summary = current_pos_summary
            if current_pos_summary is None:
                logger.warning(f"{action_context} Warning: Failed to fetch position state on attempt {attempt + 1}.")
                if attempt < max_attempts - 1: time.sleep(delay_seconds); continue
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}{action_context} FAILED: Could not fetch position after {max_attempts} attempts.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return False, last_pos_summary

            actual_is_flat, actual_open_side, actual_qty_abs = True, None, Decimal("0")
            long_pos, short_pos = current_pos_summary.get("long", {}), current_pos_summary.get("short", {})
            
            if long_pos and safe_decimal(long_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
                actual_is_flat, actual_open_side, actual_qty_abs = False, "long", safe_decimal(long_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
            elif short_pos and safe_decimal(short_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
                actual_is_flat, actual_open_side, actual_qty_abs = False, "short", safe_decimal(short_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
            
            verified, log_msg_suffix = False, ""
            if expected_side_logical is None: # Expecting flat
                verified = actual_is_flat
                log_msg_suffix = f"Expected FLAT, Actual: {'FLAT' if actual_is_flat else f'{str(actual_open_side).upper()} Qty={actual_qty_abs.normalize()}'}"
            elif actual_open_side == expected_side_logical: # Expecting specific side
                qty_ok = actual_qty_abs >= expected_qty_min_abs; verified = qty_ok
                log_msg_suffix = (f"Expected {expected_side_logical.upper()} (MinQty≈{expected_qty_min_abs.normalize()}), "
                                  f"Actual: {actual_open_side.upper()} Qty={actual_qty_abs.normalize()} ({'QTY OK' if qty_ok else 'QTY MISMATCH'})")
            else: # Side mismatch
                log_msg_suffix = (f"Expected {str(expected_side_logical).upper()}, "
                                  f"Actual: {'FLAT' if actual_is_flat else (str(actual_open_side).upper() + ' Qty=' + actual_qty_abs.normalize()) if actual_open_side else 'UNKNOWN'} (SIDE MISMATCH)")
            
            logger.debug(f"{action_context} Check {attempt + 1}: {log_msg_suffix}")
            if verified: 
                logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}{action_context} SUCCEEDED (Attempt {attempt + 1}). State: {log_msg_suffix}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return True, current_pos_summary
            if attempt < max_attempts - 1: logger.debug(f"State not as expected. Retrying in {delay_seconds}s..."); time.sleep(delay_seconds)
            else: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}{action_context} FAILED after {max_attempts} attempts. Final state: {log_msg_suffix}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return False, current_pos_summary
        return False, last_pos_summary # Fallback, should be covered by loop logic

    def place_risked_market_order(self, side: str, atr: Decimal, total_equity: Decimal, current_price: Decimal) -> bool:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.critical("OrderManager not ready for placing order."); return False
        if side not in ("buy", "sell"): logger.error(f"Invalid side '{side}' for risked order."); return False
        if atr.is_nan() or atr <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Entry Aborted: Invalid ATR for risk calc."); return False
        if total_equity is None or total_equity.is_nan() or total_equity <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Entry Aborted: Invalid Equity for risk calc."); return False
        if current_price.is_nan() or current_price <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Entry Aborted: Invalid Current Price."); return False

        logical_pos_side = "long" if side == "buy" else "short"
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.MAGENTA}--- Initiating Entry: {logical_pos_side.upper()} ---{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        
        trade_params = self._calculate_trade_parameters(side, atr, total_equity, current_price)
        if not trade_params or not trade_params.get("qty") or trade_params["qty"] <= Decimal("0"): # type: ignore
            logger.error("Entry Aborted: Failed to calculate valid trade parameters."); return False
        
        qty_order, sl_initial, tp_initial = trade_params["qty"], trade_params.get("sl_price"), trade_params.get("tp_price") # type: ignore
        if sl_initial is None or sl_initial.is_nan() or sl_initial <= Decimal("0"):
            logger.error(f"Entry Aborted: Invalid Stop Loss ({sl_initial}) from params."); return False

        market_order_info = self._execute_market_order(side, qty_order) # type: ignore
        if not market_order_info: 
            logger.error(f"Entry Aborted: Market order execution failed for {side.upper()} {qty_order.normalize()}."); self._handle_entry_failure(side, qty_order); return False # type: ignore # type: ignore
        
        order_id = market_order_info.get("id", "[N/A_ORDER_ID]")
        avg_entry_px_order_resp = safe_decimal(market_order_info.get("average", "NaN"))

        # Verify position state after order execution
        min_exp_fill_qty = qty_order * Decimal("0.90") # Expect at least 90% fill for verification # type: ignore
        verify_ok, final_pos_state = self._verify_position_state(
            expected_side_logical=logical_pos_side, expected_qty_min_abs=min_exp_fill_qty, 
            max_attempts=6, delay_seconds=max(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds, 1.0), # Generous delay
            action_context=f"Post-{logical_pos_side.upper()}-Entry Verification (Order {order_id})"
        )
        if not verify_ok: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Entry FAILED: Position verification failed after market order {order_id}. Manual check! Attempting cleanup...{Style.RESET_ALL}"); self._handle_entry_failure(side, qty_order); return False # type: ignore

        active_pos = final_pos_state.get(logical_pos_side) if final_pos_state else {} # type: ignore
        if not active_pos: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Internal Error: Position {logical_pos_side} verified OK, but details missing. Aborting.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); self._handle_entry_failure(side, qty_order); return False # type: ignore

        actual_fill_qty = safe_decimal(active_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
        actual_avg_entry_px = safe_decimal(active_pos.get("entry_price", "NaN"))
        # Use order response average price if position average price is NaN (can happen briefly)
        if actual_avg_entry_px.is_nan() and not avg_entry_px_order_resp.is_nan():
            actual_avg_entry_px = avg_entry_px_order_resp
            logger.debug(f"Used avg entry px from order response ({avg_entry_px_order_resp.normalize()}) as position data was initially NaN.")
        
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Position {logical_pos_side.upper()} CONFIRMED: Qty={actual_fill_qty.normalize()}, AvgEntryPx={actual_avg_entry_px.normalize() if not actual_avg_entry_px.is_nan() else '[N/A]'}{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        if actual_fill_qty < qty_order * Decimal("0.99"): # type: ignore
            logger.warning(f"Filled qty {actual_fill_qty.normalize()} notably less than ordered {qty_order.normalize()}. Possible slippage/partial fill.") # type: ignore

        # Set SL/TP protection
        stops_set_ok = self._set_position_protection(logical_pos_side, sl_price=sl_initial, tp_price=tp_initial)
        if not stops_set_ok:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Entry Alert: FAILED to set initial SL/TP for {logical_pos_side.upper()}. Attempting EMERGENCY CLOSE!{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            self.close_position(logical_pos_side, actual_fill_qty, reason="EmergencyClose:FailedInitialStopSet"); return False
        
        if self.config.enable_journaling: self.log_trade_entry_to_journal(side, actual_fill_qty, actual_avg_entry_px, order_id)
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}--- Entry Sequence for {logical_pos_side.upper()} Completed Successfully ---{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return True

    def manage_trailing_stop(self, position_side: str, entry_price: Decimal, current_market_price: Decimal, current_atr: Decimal):
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.error("TSL Manage: Exchange/Market info missing."); return
        
        tracker_key = position_side.lower()
        # Only manage TSL if initial SL/TP is active (i.e., TSL not yet activated)
        if self.protection_tracker.get(tracker_key) != "ACTIVE_SLTP":
            logger.debug(f"TSL Check ({position_side.upper()}): Not in ACTIVE_SLTP state (Current: {self.protection_tracker.get(tracker_key)}). Skipping."); return
        
        if any(val.is_nan() or val <= Decimal("0") for val in [current_atr, entry_price, current_market_price]): # type: ignore
            logger.debug(f"TSL Check ({position_side.upper()}): Invalid ATR/entry_px/market_px. Skipping."); return

        try:
            act_dist_pts = current_atr * self.config.tsl_activation_atr_multiplier # type: ignore
            tsl_act_target_px = entry_price + act_dist_pts if position_side == "long" else entry_price - act_dist_pts # type: ignore
            if tsl_act_target_px.is_nan() or tsl_act_target_px <= Decimal("0"):
                logger.warning(f"Invalid TSL activation price ({tsl_act_target_px.normalize()}). Skipping TSL for {position_side.upper()}."); return

            tsl_actual_dist_pts = current_market_price * (self.config.trailing_stop_percent / Decimal(100)) # type: ignore
            min_tick = self.market_info.get('tick_size', Decimal('1e-8')) # type: ignore
            if not min_tick.is_nan() and min_tick > Decimal("0") and tsl_actual_dist_pts < min_tick: # type: ignore
                logger.debug(f"TSL dist ({tsl_actual_dist_pts.normalize()}) < min tick ({min_tick.normalize()}). Adjusting."); tsl_actual_dist_pts = min_tick # type: ignore
            if tsl_actual_dist_pts <= Decimal("0"):
                logger.warning(f"Invalid TSL distance ({tsl_actual_dist_pts.normalize()}). Skipping TSL for {position_side.upper()}."); return

            should_activate = (position_side == "long" and current_market_price >= tsl_act_target_px) or \
                              (position_side == "short" and current_market_price <= tsl_act_target_px)
            
            if should_activate:
                logger.trade(f"{Fore.MAGENTA}Trailing Stop Loss (TSL) activation condition MET for {position_side.upper()}!{Style.RESET_ALL}")
                logger.trade(f"  Details: EntryPx={entry_price.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, CurrPx={current_market_price.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, TSLActTargetPx≈{tsl_act_target_px.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, TSLDistToSet≈{tsl_actual_dist_pts.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}")
                
                # Pass tsl_activation_price as the price that triggered the TSL for Bybit's `activePrice`
                # Bybit's `activePrice` is the price at which the TSL order itself becomes active.
                # For a market TSL, this is the price that, if breached, starts the trailing.
                # A common approach is to set activePrice slightly through the target, ensuring activation.
                # For simplicity, we use tsl_act_target_px, but a small buffer could be added.
                # The `tsl_distance` is the actual trail distance (e.g. percentage based).
                activation_ok = self._set_position_protection(position_side, is_tsl=True, tsl_distance=tsl_actual_dist_pts, tsl_activation_price=tsl_act_target_px) # Use target price as activation price
                if activation_ok: logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}TSL activated successfully for {position_side.upper()}.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
                else: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Failed to activate TSL for {position_side.upper()} via API.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            else:
                logger.debug(f"TSL Check ({position_side.upper()}): Activation NOT MET. (CurrPx: {current_market_price.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, TargetActPx: ~{tsl_act_target_px.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f})")
        except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Error managing TSL for {position_side.upper()}: {e}", exc_info=True)

    def close_position(self, position_side_to_close: str, qty_abs_to_close: Decimal, reason: str = "Strategy Exit Signal") -> bool:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.critical("OrderManager not ready for closing position."); return False
        if position_side_to_close not in ("long", "short"): logger.error(f"Invalid side '{position_side_to_close}' for close_position."); return False
        
        if qty_abs_to_close.is_nan() or qty_abs_to_close.copy_abs() < POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
            logger.warning(f"Close requested for zero/negligible qty ({qty_abs_to_close.normalize()}). Skipping close for {position_side_to_close.upper()}."); 
            self.protection_tracker[position_side_to_close.lower()] = None; return True # Effectively closed
        
        closing_order_side = "sell" if position_side_to_close == "long" else "buy"
        tracker_key = position_side_to_close.lower()
        logger.trade(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Attempting to CLOSE {position_side_to_close.upper()} (Qty: {qty_abs_to_close.normalize()} {self.market_info.get('base', '')}) | Reason: {reason}...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        
        logger.debug(f"Clearing any existing protection for {position_side_to_close.upper()} before closing...")
        if self._set_position_protection(position_side_to_close, sl_price=None, tp_price=None, is_tsl=False): # Clears all types
            logger.info(f"Protection cleared (or was already clear) for {position_side_to_close.upper()}."); self.protection_tracker[tracker_key] = None
        else: logger.warning(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Failed to confirm protection clear for {position_side_to_close.upper()}. Proceeding with close...{Style.RESET_ALL}")

        close_order_info = self._execute_market_order(closing_order_side, qty_abs_to_close)
        if not close_order_info:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Failed to submit closing market order for {position_side_to_close.upper()}. MANUAL INTERVENTION REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} CLOSE ORDER FAILED", f"Market {closing_order_side.upper()} order failed!"); return False
        
        close_order_id = close_order_info.get("id", "[N/A_CLOSE_ID]")
        avg_close_px = safe_decimal(close_order_info.get("average"), default=Decimal("NaN"))
        logger.trade(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Closing market order ({close_order_id}) submitted for {position_side_to_close.upper()}. Reported AvgClosePx: {avg_close_px.normalize() if not avg_close_px.is_nan() else '[Pending/N/A]'}{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Position Closing", f"{position_side_to_close.upper()} close order {close_order_id} submitted.")

        # Verify position is flat
        verify_closed_ok, _ = self._verify_position_state(
            expected_side_logical=None, # Expecting flat
            max_attempts=6, delay_seconds=max(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds + 0.5, 1.5),
            action_context=f"Post-{position_side_to_close.upper()}-Close Verification (Order {close_order_id})"
        )
        
        if self.config.enable_journaling: self.log_trade_exit_to_journal(position_side_to_close, qty_abs_to_close, avg_close_px, close_order_id, reason)
        
        if not verify_closed_ok: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Position {position_side_to_close.upper()} closure verification FAILED. Position may still be open. MANUAL INTERVENTION REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} CLOSE VERIFY FAILED", f"{position_side_to_close.upper()} pos may still be open!"); return False
        
        logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Position {position_side_to_close.upper()} confirmed closed (flat).{Style.RESET_ALL}"); self.protection_tracker[tracker_key] = None; return True

    def _handle_entry_failure(self, failed_entry_order_side: str, attempted_qty_abs: Decimal):
        logger.warning(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Handling entry failure for {failed_entry_order_side.upper()} order (intended qty: {attempted_qty_abs.normalize()}). Checking for lingering position...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        logical_pos_side_check = "long" if failed_entry_order_side == "buy" else "short"
        time.sleep(max(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds, 1.0) + 1.0) # Extra delay for propagation
        
        # Check current actual position state, expecting flat or the side we tried to enter
        _, current_pos_summary = self._verify_position_state(
            expected_side_logical=None, # Check if flat, or if a position exists
            max_attempts=2, delay_seconds=1.0, 
            action_context=f"Entry-Failure-Cleanup-Check-{logical_pos_side_check.upper()}"
        )
        if current_pos_summary is None:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Could not fetch positions during entry failure handling for {logical_pos_side_check.upper()}. MANUAL CHECK URGENTLY REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} URGENT CHECK", "Pos fetch failed during entry cleanup!"); return

        lingering_pos = current_pos_summary.get(logical_pos_side_check, {})
        current_linger_qty = safe_decimal(lingering_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
        
        if current_linger_qty >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} position (Qty: {current_linger_qty.normalize()}) found after failed entry. Attempting emergency close...{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Emergency Close", f"Lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} pos found.")
            if self.close_position(logical_pos_side_check, current_linger_qty, reason="EmergencyClose:LingeringAfterEntryFail"):
                logger.info(f"Emergency close for lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} position submitted/confirmed.")
            else:
                logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}EMERGENCY CLOSE FAILED for lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()}. MANUAL INTERVENTION URGENTLY REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} URGENT CHECK", f"Emergency close of lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} FAILED!")
        else: 
            logger.info(f"No significant lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} position detected (Current qty: {current_linger_qty.normalize()})."); self.protection_tracker[logical_pos_side_check] = None

    def _write_journal_row(self, trade_data: Dict[str, Any]):
        if not self.config.enable_journaling: return
        journal_file = Path(self.config.journal_file_path)
        file_exists_has_content = journal_file.is_file() and journal_file.stat().st_size > 0
        try:
            journal_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True) # Ensure directory exists
            with journal_file.open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as csvfile:
                fieldnames = ["TimestampUTC", "Symbol", "Action", "Side", "Quantity", "AvgPrice", "OrderID", "Reason", "Notes"]
                writer = csv.DictWriter(csvfile, fieldnames=fieldnames, quoting=csv.QUOTE_MINIMAL)
                if not file_exists_has_content: writer.writeheader()
                
                row_to_write = {}
                for field in fieldnames:
