                symbol=self.config.symbol, side=side, amount=amount_float_ccxt, params=params_v5,
                max_retries=self.config.max_fetch_retries, delay_seconds=self.config.retry_delay_seconds
            )
            if order_resp is None: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order submission failed (returned None).{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None

            order_id, status = order_resp.get("id","[N/A]"), order_resp.get("status","[unknown]")
            filled_qty, avg_px = safe_decimal(order_resp.get("filled","0")), safe_decimal(order_resp.get("average","0"))
            avg_px_log = avg_px.normalize() if not avg_px.is_nan() and avg_px > Decimal("0") else "[N/A]"
            
            logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Market order submitted: ID {order_id}, Side {side.upper()}, Ordered {final_qty_dec_log.normalize()}, Status: {status}, Filled: {filled_qty.normalize()}, AvgPx: {avg_px_log}{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Order Submitted", f"Market {side.upper()} {final_qty_dec_log.normalize()} ID:{order_id}, Status:{status}")

            if status == "rejected": 
                reject_reason = order_resp.get('info', {}).get('rejectReason', order_resp.get('info', {}).get('retMsg', 'No reason provided'))
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order {order_id} REJECTED. Reason: '{reject_reason}'. Info: {order_resp.get('info')}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
            if status == "canceled" and filled_qty == Decimal("0") and params_v5.get("timeInForce") == "ImmediateOrCancel":
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order {order_id} (IOC) CANCELED with 0 filled. No execution.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
            if status == "expired": logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Market order {order_id} EXPIRED. Unexpected for market.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return None
            
            logger.debug(f"Delay ({self.config.order_check_delay_seconds}s) after market order {order_id} for propagation..."); time.sleep(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds)
            return order_resp
        except (ccxt.InsufficientFunds, ccxt.InvalidOrder) as e: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Order placement failed ({type(e).__name__}): {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=False)
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Order FAILED", f"Market {side.upper()} failed: {str(e)[:50]}"); return None
        except Exception as e: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Unexpected error placing market order: {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True)
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Order ERROR", f"Market {side.upper()} unexpected error."); return None

    def _set_position_protection(self, position_side: str, sl_price: Optional[Decimal] = None, tp_price: Optional[Decimal] = None, is_tsl: bool = False, tsl_distance: Optional[Decimal] = None, tsl_activation_price: Optional[Decimal] = None) -> bool:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.error("Protection: Exchange/Market info missing."); return False
        market_id = self.market_info.get("id")
        if not market_id: logger.error("Protection: Market ID missing."); return False
        
        tracker_key = position_side.lower() # "long" or "short"
        if tracker_key not in self.protection_tracker: logger.error(f"Invalid position_side '{position_side}' for protection tracker."); return False

        # Format parameters for API: "0" means clear/disable that specific stop type
        sl_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(sl_price, "price", allow_zero=True) if sl_price else "0"
        tp_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(tp_price, "price", allow_zero=True) if tp_price else "0"
        tsl_dist_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(tsl_distance, "distance", allow_zero=False) if tsl_distance else "0" # TSL dist cannot be 0 if active
        tsl_act_px_api_str = self.exchange_manager._format_v5_param(tsl_activation_price, "price", allow_zero=False) if tsl_activation_price else "0" # Activation px cannot be 0

        api_params: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "category": self.config.bybit_v5_category, 
            "symbol": market_id, 
            "positionIdx": self.config.position_idx, 
            "tpslMode": V5_TPSL_MODE_FULL # Assumes "Full" TP/SL mode for the entire position
        }
        action_desc, new_tracker_state = "", None

        if is_tsl:
            if tsl_dist_api_str and tsl_dist_api_str != "0" and tsl_act_px_api_str and tsl_act_px_api_str != "0":
                api_params.update({
                    "trailingStop": tsl_dist_api_str, 
                    "activePrice": tsl_act_px_api_str, # Activation price for TSL
                    "triggerBy": self.config.tsl_trigger_by,
                    "stopLoss": "0", "takeProfit": "0" # Clear regular SL/TP when TSL is active
                })
                action_desc, new_tracker_state = f"ACTIVATE/MODIFY TSL (Dist: {tsl_dist_api_str}, ActPx: {tsl_act_px_api_str})", "ACTIVE_TSL"
            else: logger.error(f"Cannot activate TSL for {position_side.upper()}: Invalid TSL distance ('{tsl_dist_api_str}') or act price ('{tsl_act_px_api_str}')."); return False
        elif sl_api_str != "0" or tp_api_str != "0": # Setting SL and/or TP
            if sl_api_str != "0": api_params["stopLoss"] = sl_api_str
            if tp_api_str != "0": api_params["takeProfit"] = tp_api_str
            api_params.update({ # Ensure trigger types are set, and TSL is cleared
                "slTriggerBy": self.config.sl_trigger_by, 
                "tpTriggerBy": self.config.sl_trigger_by, # Bybit uses same trigger for TP as SL by default
                "trailingStop": "0", "activePrice": "0" # Clear TSL if setting SL/TP
            })
            action_desc, new_tracker_state = f"SET SL={api_params.get('stopLoss','0')} TP={api_params.get('takeProfit','0')}", "ACTIVE_SLTP"
        else: # Clearing all protection
            api_params.update({"stopLoss": "0", "takeProfit": "0", "trailingStop": "0", "activePrice": "0"})
            action_desc, new_tracker_state = "CLEAR ALL SL/TP/TSL", None
        
        logger.trade(f"{Fore.CYAN}Attempting to {action_desc} for {position_side.upper()} {self.config.symbol}...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        logger.debug(f"Calling V5 setTradingStop with params: {api_params}")
        
        # This uses a private method name from CCXT; this can be fragile if CCXT changes internals.
        # A comment highlighting this specific dependency is good.
        # TODO: Monitor CCXT for a standardized public method for v5/position/trading-stop.
        private_method_name = "privatePostPositionTradingStop" 
        if not hasattr(self.exchange, private_method_name): 
            logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}Fatal: CCXT method '{private_method_name}' not found. Cannot manage position protection.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return False
        
        method_to_call = getattr(self.exchange, private_method_name)
        try:
            response = fetch_with_retries(method_to_call, params=api_params, max_retries=self.config.max_fetch_retries, delay_seconds=self.config.retry_delay_seconds)
            if response and response.get("retCode") == V5_SUCCESS_RETCODE:
                logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}{action_desc} successful for {position_side.upper()}.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
                termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Protection {('Set' if new_tracker_state else 'Cleared')}", f"{action_desc} for {position_side.upper()}")
                self.protection_tracker[tracker_key] = new_tracker_state; return True
            else:
                ret_code = response.get("retCode", "[N/A]") if response else "[No Resp]"
                ret_msg = response.get("retMsg", "[No Msg]") if response else "[No Resp]"
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}{action_desc} failed for {position_side.upper()}. API: Code={ret_code}, Msg='{ret_msg}'.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); logger.debug(f"Full response from failed {private_method_name}: {response}")
                termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Protection FAILED", f"{action_desc[:30]}... failed: {ret_msg[:50]}"); return False
        except Exception as e: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Unexpected error during '{action_desc}' for {position_side.upper()}: {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True)
            termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Protection ERROR", f"{action_desc[:30]}... error."); return False

    def _verify_position_state(self, expected_side_logical: Optional[str], expected_qty_min_abs: Decimal = POSITION_QTY_EPSILON, max_attempts: int = 4, delay_seconds: float = 1.5, action_context: str = "Position Verification") -> Tuple[bool, Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]]:
        logger.debug(f"{action_context}: Verifying. Expect side: '{expected_side_logical}', MinQty≈{expected_qty_min_abs.normalize()}. Attempts: {max_attempts}.")
        last_pos_summary: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]] = None
        for attempt in range(max_attempts):
            logger.debug(f"{action_context}: Attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_attempts}...")
            current_pos_summary = self.exchange_manager.get_current_position(); last_pos_summary = current_pos_summary
            if current_pos_summary is None:
                logger.warning(f"{action_context} Warning: Failed to fetch position state on attempt {attempt + 1}.")
                if attempt < max_attempts - 1: time.sleep(delay_seconds); continue
                logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}{action_context} FAILED: Could not fetch position after {max_attempts} attempts.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return False, last_pos_summary

            actual_is_flat, actual_open_side, actual_qty_abs = True, None, Decimal("0")
            long_pos, short_pos = current_pos_summary.get("long", {}), current_pos_summary.get("short", {})
            
            if long_pos and safe_decimal(long_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
                actual_is_flat, actual_open_side, actual_qty_abs = False, "long", safe_decimal(long_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
            elif short_pos and safe_decimal(short_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
                actual_is_flat, actual_open_side, actual_qty_abs = False, "short", safe_decimal(short_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
            
            verified, log_msg_suffix = False, ""
            if expected_side_logical is None: # Expecting flat
                verified = actual_is_flat
                log_msg_suffix = f"Expected FLAT, Actual: {'FLAT' if actual_is_flat else f'{str(actual_open_side).upper()} Qty={actual_qty_abs.normalize()}'}"
            elif actual_open_side == expected_side_logical: # Expecting specific side
                qty_ok = actual_qty_abs >= expected_qty_min_abs; verified = qty_ok
                log_msg_suffix = (f"Expected {expected_side_logical.upper()} (MinQty≈{expected_qty_min_abs.normalize()}), "
                                  f"Actual: {actual_open_side.upper()} Qty={actual_qty_abs.normalize()} ({'QTY OK' if qty_ok else 'QTY MISMATCH'})")
            else: # Side mismatch
                log_msg_suffix = (f"Expected {str(expected_side_logical).upper()}, "
                                  f"Actual: {'FLAT' if actual_is_flat else (str(actual_open_side).upper() + ' Qty=' + actual_qty_abs.normalize()) if actual_open_side else 'UNKNOWN'} (SIDE MISMATCH)")
            
            logger.debug(f"{action_context} Check {attempt + 1}: {log_msg_suffix}")
            if verified: 
                logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}{action_context} SUCCEEDED (Attempt {attempt + 1}). State: {log_msg_suffix}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return True, current_pos_summary
            if attempt < max_attempts - 1: logger.debug(f"State not as expected. Retrying in {delay_seconds}s..."); time.sleep(delay_seconds)
            else: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}{action_context} FAILED after {max_attempts} attempts. Final state: {log_msg_suffix}{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return False, current_pos_summary
        return False, last_pos_summary # Fallback, should be covered by loop logic

    def place_risked_market_order(self, side: str, atr: Decimal, total_equity: Decimal, current_price: Decimal) -> bool:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.critical("OrderManager not ready for placing order."); return False
        if side not in ("buy", "sell"): logger.error(f"Invalid side '{side}' for risked order."); return False
        if atr.is_nan() or atr <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Entry Aborted: Invalid ATR for risk calc."); return False
        if total_equity is None or total_equity.is_nan() or total_equity <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Entry Aborted: Invalid Equity for risk calc."); return False
        if current_price.is_nan() or current_price <= Decimal("0"): logger.error("Entry Aborted: Invalid Current Price."); return False

        logical_pos_side = "long" if side == "buy" else "short"
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.MAGENTA}--- Initiating Entry: {logical_pos_side.upper()} ---{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        
        trade_params = self._calculate_trade_parameters(side, atr, total_equity, current_price)
        if not trade_params or not trade_params.get("qty") or trade_params["qty"] <= Decimal("0"): # type: ignore
            logger.error("Entry Aborted: Failed to calculate valid trade parameters."); return False
        
        qty_order, sl_initial, tp_initial = trade_params["qty"], trade_params.get("sl_price"), trade_params.get("tp_price") # type: ignore
        if sl_initial is None or sl_initial.is_nan() or sl_initial <= Decimal("0"):
            logger.error(f"Entry Aborted: Invalid Stop Loss ({sl_initial}) from params."); return False

        market_order_info = self._execute_market_order(side, qty_order) # type: ignore
        if not market_order_info: 
            logger.error(f"Entry Aborted: Market order execution failed for {side.upper()} {qty_order.normalize()}."); self._handle_entry_failure(side, qty_order); return False # type: ignore # type: ignore
        
        order_id = market_order_info.get("id", "[N/A_ORDER_ID]")
        avg_entry_px_order_resp = safe_decimal(market_order_info.get("average", "NaN"))

        # Verify position state after order execution
        min_exp_fill_qty = qty_order * Decimal("0.90") # Expect at least 90% fill for verification # type: ignore
        verify_ok, final_pos_state = self._verify_position_state(
            expected_side_logical=logical_pos_side, expected_qty_min_abs=min_exp_fill_qty, 
            max_attempts=6, delay_seconds=max(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds, 1.0), # Generous delay
            action_context=f"Post-{logical_pos_side.upper()}-Entry Verification (Order {order_id})"
        )
        if not verify_ok: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Entry FAILED: Position verification failed after market order {order_id}. Manual check! Attempting cleanup...{Style.RESET_ALL}"); self._handle_entry_failure(side, qty_order); return False # type: ignore

        active_pos = final_pos_state.get(logical_pos_side) if final_pos_state else {} # type: ignore
        if not active_pos: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Internal Error: Position {logical_pos_side} verified OK, but details missing. Aborting.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); self._handle_entry_failure(side, qty_order); return False # type: ignore

        actual_fill_qty = safe_decimal(active_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
        actual_avg_entry_px = safe_decimal(active_pos.get("entry_price", "NaN"))
        # Use order response average price if position average price is NaN (can happen briefly)
        if actual_avg_entry_px.is_nan() and not avg_entry_px_order_resp.is_nan():
            actual_avg_entry_px = avg_entry_px_order_resp
            logger.debug(f"Used avg entry px from order response ({avg_entry_px_order_resp.normalize()}) as position data was initially NaN.")
        
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Position {logical_pos_side.upper()} CONFIRMED: Qty={actual_fill_qty.normalize()}, AvgEntryPx={actual_avg_entry_px.normalize() if not actual_avg_entry_px.is_nan() else '[N/A]'}{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        if actual_fill_qty < qty_order * Decimal("0.99"): # type: ignore
            logger.warning(f"Filled qty {actual_fill_qty.normalize()} notably less than ordered {qty_order.normalize()}. Possible slippage/partial fill.") # type: ignore

        # Set SL/TP protection
        stops_set_ok = self._set_position_protection(logical_pos_side, sl_price=sl_initial, tp_price=tp_initial)
        if not stops_set_ok:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Entry Alert: FAILED to set initial SL/TP for {logical_pos_side.upper()}. Attempting EMERGENCY CLOSE!{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            self.close_position(logical_pos_side, actual_fill_qty, reason="EmergencyClose:FailedInitialStopSet"); return False
        
        if self.config.enable_journaling: self.log_trade_entry_to_journal(side, actual_fill_qty, actual_avg_entry_px, order_id)
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}--- Entry Sequence for {logical_pos_side.upper()} Completed Successfully ---{Style.RESET_ALL}"); return True

    def manage_trailing_stop(self, position_side: str, entry_price: Decimal, current_market_price: Decimal, current_atr: Decimal):
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.error("TSL Manage: Exchange/Market info missing."); return
        
        tracker_key = position_side.lower()
        # Only manage TSL if initial SL/TP is active (i.e., TSL not yet activated)
        if self.protection_tracker.get(tracker_key) != "ACTIVE_SLTP":
            logger.debug(f"TSL Check ({position_side.upper()}): Not in ACTIVE_SLTP state (Current: {self.protection_tracker.get(tracker_key)}). Skipping."); return
        
        if any(val.is_nan() or val <= Decimal("0") for val in [current_atr, entry_price, current_market_price]): # type: ignore
            logger.debug(f"TSL Check ({position_side.upper()}): Invalid ATR/entry_px/market_px. Skipping."); return

        try:
            act_dist_pts = current_atr * self.config.tsl_activation_atr_multiplier # type: ignore
            tsl_act_target_px = entry_price + act_dist_pts if position_side == "long" else entry_price - act_dist_pts # type: ignore
            if tsl_act_target_px.is_nan() or tsl_act_target_px <= Decimal("0"):
                logger.warning(f"Invalid TSL activation price ({tsl_act_target_px.normalize()}). Skipping TSL for {position_side.upper()}."); return

            tsl_actual_dist_pts = current_market_price * (self.config.trailing_stop_percent / Decimal(100)) # type: ignore
            min_tick = self.market_info.get('tick_size', Decimal('1e-8')) # type: ignore
            if not min_tick.is_nan() and min_tick > Decimal("0") and tsl_actual_dist_pts < min_tick: # type: ignore
                logger.debug(f"TSL dist ({tsl_actual_dist_pts.normalize()}) < min tick ({min_tick.normalize()}). Adjusting."); tsl_actual_dist_pts = min_tick # type: ignore
            if tsl_actual_dist_pts <= Decimal("0"):
                logger.warning(f"Invalid TSL distance ({tsl_actual_dist_pts.normalize()}). Skipping TSL for {position_side.upper()}."); return

            should_activate = (position_side == "long" and current_market_price >= tsl_act_target_px) or \
                              (position_side == "short" and current_market_price <= tsl_act_target_px)
            
            if should_activate:
                logger.trade(f"{Fore.MAGENTA}Trailing Stop Loss (TSL) activation condition MET for {position_side.upper()}!{Style.RESET_ALL}")
                logger.trade(f"  Details: EntryPx={entry_price.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, CurrPx={current_market_price.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, TSLActTargetPx≈{tsl_act_target_px.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, TSLDistToSet≈{tsl_actual_dist_pts.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}")
                
                # Pass tsl_activation_price as the price that triggered the TSL for Bybit's `activePrice`
                # Bybit's `activePrice` is the price at which the TSL order itself becomes active.
                # For a market TSL, this is the price that, if breached, starts the trailing.
                # A common approach is to set activePrice slightly through the target, ensuring activation.
                # For simplicity, we use tsl_act_target_px, but a small buffer could be added.
                # The `tsl_distance` is the actual trail distance (e.g. percentage based).
                activation_ok = self._set_position_protection(position_side, is_tsl=True, tsl_distance=tsl_actual_dist_pts, tsl_activation_price=tsl_act_target_px) # Use target price as activation price
                if activation_ok: logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}TSL activated successfully for {position_side.upper()}.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
                else: logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Failed to activate TSL for {position_side.upper()} via API.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            else:
                logger.debug(f"TSL Check ({position_side.upper()}): Activation NOT MET. (CurrPx: {current_market_price.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f}, TargetActPx: ~{tsl_act_target_px.normalize():.{DEFAULT_PRICE_DP}f})")
        except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Error managing TSL for {position_side.upper()}: {e}", exc_info=True)

    def close_position(self, position_side_to_close: str, qty_abs_to_close: Decimal, reason: str = "Strategy Exit Signal") -> bool:
        if not self.exchange or not self.market_info: logger.critical("OrderManager not ready for closing position."); return False
        if position_side_to_close not in ("long", "short"): logger.error(f"Invalid side '{position_side_to_close}' for close_position."); return False
        
        if qty_abs_to_close.is_nan() or qty_abs_to_close.copy_abs() < POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
            logger.warning(f"Close requested for zero/negligible qty ({qty_abs_to_close.normalize()}). Skipping close for {position_side_to_close.upper()}."); 
            self.protection_tracker[position_side_to_close.lower()] = None; return True # Effectively closed
        
        closing_order_side = "sell" if position_side_to_close == "long" else "buy"
        tracker_key = position_side_to_close.lower()
        logger.trade(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Attempting to CLOSE {position_side_to_close.upper()} (Qty: {qty_abs_to_close.normalize()} {self.market_info.get('base', '')}) | Reason: {reason}...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        
        logger.debug(f"Clearing any existing protection for {position_side_to_close.upper()} before closing...")
        if self._set_position_protection(position_side_to_close, sl_price=None, tp_price=None, is_tsl=False): # Clears all types
            logger.info(f"Protection cleared (or was already clear) for {position_side_to_close.upper()}."); self.protection_tracker[tracker_key] = None
        else: logger.warning(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Failed to confirm protection clear for {position_side_to_close.upper()}. Proceeding with close...{Style.RESET_ALL}")

        close_order_info = self._execute_market_order(closing_order_side, qty_abs_to_close)
        if not close_order_info:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Failed to submit closing market order for {position_side_to_close.upper()}. MANUAL INTERVENTION REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} CLOSE ORDER FAILED", f"Market {closing_order_side.upper()} order failed!"); return False
        
        close_order_id = close_order_info.get("id", "[N/A_CLOSE_ID]")
        avg_close_px = safe_decimal(close_order_info.get("average"), default=Decimal("NaN"))
        logger.trade(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Closing market order ({close_order_id}) submitted for {position_side_to_close.upper()}. Reported AvgClosePx: {avg_close_px.normalize() if not avg_close_px.is_nan() else '[Pending/N/A]'}{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Position Closing", f"{position_side_to_close.upper()} close order {close_order_id} submitted.")

        # Verify position is flat
        verify_closed_ok, _ = self._verify_position_state(
            expected_side_logical=None, # Expecting flat
            max_attempts=6, delay_seconds=max(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds + 0.5, 1.5),
            action_context=f"Post-{position_side_to_close.upper()}-Close Verification (Order {close_order_id})"
        )
        
        if self.config.enable_journaling: self.log_trade_exit_to_journal(position_side_to_close, qty_abs_to_close, avg_close_px, close_order_id, reason)
        
        if not verify_closed_ok: 
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Position {position_side_to_close.upper()} closure verification FAILED. Position may still be open. MANUAL INTERVENTION REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} CLOSE VERIFY FAILED", f"{position_side_to_close.upper()} pos may still be open!"); return False
        
        logger.trade(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Position {position_side_to_close.upper()} confirmed closed (flat).{Style.RESET_ALL}"); self.protection_tracker[tracker_key] = None; return True

    def _handle_entry_failure(self, failed_entry_order_side: str, attempted_qty_abs: Decimal):
        logger.warning(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Handling entry failure for {failed_entry_order_side.upper()} order (intended qty: {attempted_qty_abs.normalize()}). Checking for lingering position...{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        logical_pos_side_check = "long" if failed_entry_order_side == "buy" else "short"
        time.sleep(max(self.config.order_check_delay_seconds, 1.0) + 1.0) # Extra delay for propagation
        
        # Check current actual position state, expecting flat or the side we tried to enter
        _, current_pos_summary = self._verify_position_state(
            expected_side_logical=None, # Check if flat, or if a position exists
            max_attempts=2, delay_seconds=1.0, 
            action_context=f"Entry-Failure-Cleanup-Check-{logical_pos_side_check.upper()}"
        )
        if current_pos_summary is None:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Could not fetch positions during entry failure handling for {logical_pos_side_check.upper()}. MANUAL CHECK URGENTLY REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} URGENT CHECK", "Pos fetch failed during entry cleanup!"); return

        lingering_pos = current_pos_summary.get(logical_pos_side_check, {})
        current_linger_qty = safe_decimal(lingering_pos.get("qty", "0")).copy_abs()
        
        if current_linger_qty >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} position (Qty: {current_linger_qty.normalize()}) found after failed entry. Attempting emergency close...{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} Emergency Close", f"Lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} pos found.")
            if self.close_position(logical_pos_side_check, current_linger_qty, reason="EmergencyClose:LingeringAfterEntryFail"):
                logger.info(f"Emergency close for lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} position submitted/confirmed.")
            else:
                logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}EMERGENCY CLOSE FAILED for lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()}. MANUAL INTERVENTION URGENTLY REQUIRED!{Style.RESET_ALL}"); termux_notify(f"{self.config.symbol} URGENT CHECK", f"Emergency close of lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} FAILED!")
        else: 
            logger.info(f"No significant lingering {logical_pos_side_check.upper()} position detected (Current qty: {current_linger_qty.normalize()})."); self.protection_tracker[logical_pos_side_check] = None

    def _write_journal_row(self, trade_data: Dict[str, Any]):
        if not self.config.enable_journaling: return
        journal_file = Path(self.config.journal_file_path)
        file_exists_has_content = journal_file.is_file() and journal_file.stat().st_size > 0
        try:
            journal_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True) # Ensure directory exists
            with journal_file.open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as csvfile:
                fieldnames = ["TimestampUTC", "Symbol", "Action", "Side", "Quantity", "AvgPrice", "OrderID", "Reason", "Notes"]
                writer = csv.DictWriter(csvfile, fieldnames=fieldnames, quoting=csv.QUOTE_MINIMAL)
                if not file_exists_has_content: writer.writeheader()
                
                row_to_write = {}
                for field in fieldnames:
                    val = trade_data.get(field)
                    if isinstance(val, Decimal):
                        row_to_write[field] = 'NaN' if val.is_nan() else str(val.normalize())
                    else:
                        row_to_write[field] = str(val if val is not None else 'N/A')
                row_to_write['Notes'] = str(trade_data.get('Notes', '')) # Ensure Notes is string, default empty

                writer.writerow(row_to_write)
            logger.debug(f"Trade action '{trade_data.get('Action', 'Unknown')}' logged to journal: {journal_file.name}")
        except IOError as e: logger.error(f"I/O error writing to journal '{journal_file.name}': {e}")
        except Exception as e: logger.error(f"Unexpected error writing to journal: {e}", exc_info=True)

    def log_trade_entry_to_journal(self, order_side: str, filled_qty_abs: Decimal, avg_fill_price: Decimal, order_id: Optional[str]):
        self._write_journal_row({
            "TimestampUTC": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), 
            "Symbol": self.config.symbol, "Action": "ENTRY", 
            "Side": ("long" if order_side == "buy" else "short").upper(), 
            "Quantity": filled_qty_abs, "AvgPrice": avg_fill_price, 
            "OrderID": order_id, "Reason": "Strategy Entry Signal"
        })

    def log_trade_exit_to_journal(self, position_side_closed: str, closed_qty_abs: Decimal, avg_close_price: Decimal, order_id: Optional[str], exit_reason: str):
        self._write_journal_row({
            "TimestampUTC": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), 
            "Symbol": self.config.symbol, "Action": "EXIT", 
            "Side": position_side_closed.upper(), 
            "Quantity": closed_qty_abs, "AvgPrice": avg_close_price, 
            "OrderID": order_id, "Reason": exit_reason
        })

# --- Status Display Class ---
class StatusDisplay:
    def __init__(self, config: TradingConfig):
        self.config = config
        self._default_price_dp_display = DEFAULT_PRICE_DP
        self._default_amount_dp_display = DEFAULT_AMOUNT_DP

    def _format_decimal_for_rich(self, value: Optional[Decimal], precision: Optional[int] = None, default_precision_fallback: int = 2, add_commas: bool = False, highlight_negative: bool = False, default_style: str = "white", style_override: Optional[str] = None) -> Text:
        if value is None or (isinstance(value, Decimal) and value.is_nan()): return Text("N/A", style="dim")
        
        dp_to_use = precision if precision is not None else default_precision_fallback
        try:
            # Quantize to the desired decimal places
            quantizer = Decimal('1').scaleb(-dp_to_use)
            fmt_dec_val = value.quantize(quantizer, rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)
            
            # Format with or without commas
            format_spec = f"{{:{',' if add_commas else ''}.{dp_to_use}f}}"
            display_str = format_spec.format(fmt_dec_val)
            
            current_style = style_override if style_override else default_style
            if highlight_negative and not style_override: # Only apply if no specific style override
                if fmt_dec_val < Decimal("0"): current_style = "bright_red"
                elif fmt_dec_val > Decimal("0"): current_style = "bright_green" # Positive PnL, etc.
            return Text(display_str, style=current_style)
        except (ValueError, TypeError, InvalidOperation) as e: 
            logger.error(f"Error formatting decimal '{value}' for Rich: {e}"); return Text("ERR", style="bold bright_red")

    def print_status_panel(self, cycle_num: int, current_timestamp: Optional[datetime], current_market_price: Optional[Decimal], indicators_data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]], current_positions_summary: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]], account_equity: Optional[Decimal], signal_check_result: Dict[str, Any], protection_status_tracker: Dict[str, Optional[str]], market_specific_info: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]):
        price_dp = self._default_price_dp_display
        amount_dp = self._default_amount_dp_display
        if market_specific_info and "precision_dp" in market_specific_info:
             price_dp = market_specific_info["precision_dp"].get("price", self._default_price_dp_display)
             amount_dp = market_specific_info["precision_dp"].get("amount", self._default_amount_dp_display)

        panel_content = Text()
        ts_str = current_timestamp.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z") if current_timestamp else Text("Timestamp N/A", style="dim").plain
        panel_title = f" Cycle {cycle_num} | {self.config.symbol} ({self.config.interval}) | {ts_str} "
        settle_ccy = market_specific_info.get("settle", "SETTLE") if market_specific_info else "SETTLE"
        
        panel_content.append("Price: ", style="bold bright_cyan"); panel_content.append(self._format_decimal_for_rich(current_market_price, price_dp, style_override="bright_white"))
        panel_content.append(" | Equity: ", style="bold bright_yellow"); panel_content.append(self._format_decimal_for_rich(account_equity, 2, add_commas=True, style_override="bright_yellow")); panel_content.append(f" {settle_ccy}\n", style="bright_yellow")
        
        # Indicators Section (Multi-line for clarity)
        panel_content.append("Indicators:\n", style="bold bright_cyan")
        if indicators_data:
            def fmt_ind(key: str, prec: int = 1, style: str = "white", is_bool: bool = False, true_style: Optional[str] = None, false_style: Optional[str] = None) -> Text:
                 val = indicators_data.get(key)
                 if is_bool:
                     true_s = true_style or style
                     false_s = false_style or f"dim {style}"
                     return Text(str(val), style=(true_s if val else false_s))
                 if isinstance(val, bool): return Text(str(val), style=style) # Should be handled by is_bool
                 dec_val = val if isinstance(val, Decimal) else safe_decimal(str(val) if val is not None else "NaN")
                 return self._format_decimal_for_rich(dec_val, precision=prec, default_style=style)

            # Original Indicators
            orig_parts = []
            orig_parts.append(Text("EMA(F/S/T):").append(fmt_ind('fast_ema', price_dp, "cyan")).append("/").append(fmt_ind('slow_ema', price_dp, "magenta")).append("/").append(fmt_ind('trend_ema', price_dp, "yellow")))
            stoch_text = Text("Stoch(K/D/PrK):").append(fmt_ind('stoch_k',1,"bright_blue")).append("/").append(fmt_ind('stoch_d',1,"blue")).append("/").append(fmt_ind('stoch_k_prev',1,"dim blue"))
            if indicators_data.get('stoch_kd_bullish'): stoch_text.append(" [b green]▲BullX[/b green]")
            elif indicators_data.get('stoch_kd_bearish'): stoch_text.append(" [b red]▼BearX[/b red]")
            orig_parts.append(stoch_text)
            orig_parts.append(Text(f"ATR({indicators_data.get('atr_period',self.config.atr_period)}):").append(fmt_ind('atr', price_dp+1, "bright_magenta")))
            adx_val = indicators_data.get('adx', Decimal("NaN")); adx_val = adx_val if isinstance(adx_val, Decimal) else safe_decimal(str(adx_val)) # type: ignore
            adx_style = "yellow" if not adx_val.is_nan() and adx_val > self.config.min_adx_level else "dim yellow"
            orig_parts.append(Text(f"ADX({self.config.adx_period}):").append(self._format_decimal_for_rich(adx_val,1,default_style=adx_style)).append(" [+DI:",style="dim").append(fmt_ind('pdi',1,"bright_green")).append(" -DI:",style="dim").append(fmt_ind('mdi',1,"bright_red")).append("]",style="dim"))
            panel_content.append("  Orig: ", style="yellow"); panel_content.append(Text(" | ", style="dim").join(orig_parts)); panel_content.append("\n")

            # VolumaticTrend Indicators
            if self.config.vt_enable and indicators_data.get('vt_trend_ema') is not None:
                vt_parts = []
                vt_parts.append(Text("TrendEMA:").append(fmt_ind('vt_trend_ema', price_dp, "green")))
                vt_parts.append(Text("VWMA:").append(fmt_ind('vt_vwma', price_dp, "green")))
                vt_parts.append(Text("VolSpike:").append(fmt_ind('vt_is_volume_spike', style="green", is_bool=True, true_style="green", false_style="dim green")))
                cdl_color = "Green" if indicators_data.get('vt_candle_is_green') else "Red" if indicators_data.get('vt_candle_is_red') else "Neutral"
                cdl_style = "green" if cdl_color=="Green" else "red" if cdl_color=="Red" else "dim"
                vt_parts.append(Text(f"Candle:{cdl_color}", style=cdl_style))
                panel_content.append("  VT:   ", style="green"); panel_content.append(Text(" | ", style="dim").join(vt_parts)); panel_content.append("\n")
        else: panel_content.append(Text("  Calculating or data unavailable...\n", style="dim"))

        # Position Section
        panel_content.append("Position: ", style="bold bright_cyan"); pos_display = Text("FLAT", style="bold bright_green")
        active_pos_side: Optional[str] = None; active_pos_data: Optional[Dict[str,Any]] = None
        if current_positions_summary: # Check if dict is not None
            long_data, short_data = current_positions_summary.get("long",{}), current_positions_summary.get("short",{})
            if long_data and safe_decimal(long_data.get("qty")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON: active_pos_side, active_pos_data = "long", long_data
            elif short_data and safe_decimal(short_data.get("qty")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON: active_pos_side, active_pos_data = "short", short_data
        
        if active_pos_side and active_pos_data:
            style = "bold bright_green" if active_pos_side == "long" else "bold bright_red"
            pos_display = Text(f"{active_pos_side.upper()}: ", style=style)
            pos_display.append("Qty=",style=style).append(self._format_decimal_for_rich(active_pos_data.get("qty"),amount_dp))
            pos_display.append(" | EntryPx=",style="dim").append(self._format_decimal_for_rich(active_pos_data.get("entry_price"),price_dp))
            pos_display.append(" | PnL=",style="dim").append(self._format_decimal_for_rich(active_pos_data.get("unrealized_pnl"),4,add_commas=True,highlight_negative=True))
            
            prot_text = Text(" | Protection: ",style="dim"); exch_prot_parts = []
            sl, tp, tsl_active, tsl_trig_px = active_pos_data.get("stop_loss_price"), active_pos_data.get("take_profit_price"), active_pos_data.get("is_tsl_active",False), active_pos_data.get("tsl_trigger_price")
            if tsl_active:
                exch_prot_parts.append(Text("TSL",style="bright_magenta"))
                if tsl_trig_px: exch_prot_parts.append(Text(f"(ActPx:{self._format_decimal_for_rich(tsl_trig_px,price_dp).plain})",style="magenta"))
            elif sl or tp:
                if sl: exch_prot_parts.append(Text(f"SL:{self._format_decimal_for_rich(sl,price_dp).plain}",style="bright_yellow"))
                if tp: exch_prot_parts.append(Text(f"TP:{self._format_decimal_for_rich(tp,price_dp).plain}",style="bright_yellow"))
            if not exch_prot_parts: exch_prot_parts.append(Text("None",style="dim"))
            
            prot_text.append("Exch:").append(Text(" ").join(exch_prot_parts))
            local_tracker_status = protection_status_tracker.get(active_pos_side)
            prot_text.append(" LocalTrk:").append(Text(str(local_tracker_status) if local_tracker_status else "None",style="blue" if local_tracker_status else "dim"))
            # Basic mismatch check (can be more sophisticated)
            is_mismatch = (tsl_active and local_tracker_status != "ACTIVE_TSL") or \
                          ((sl or tp) and not tsl_active and local_tracker_status != "ACTIVE_SLTP") or \
                          (not tsl_active and not sl and not tp and local_tracker_status is not None)
            if is_mismatch: prot_text.append(Text(" [TrackerMismatch?]",style="bold bright_yellow"))
            pos_display.append(prot_text)
        panel_content.append(pos_display); panel_content.append("\n")

        # Signal/Status Section (Multi-line using structured reasons)
        panel_content.append("Signal/Status:\n", style="bold bright_cyan")
        summary_reason = str(signal_check_result.get("summary","No status info"))
        status_style = "dim" # Default style
        if signal_check_result.get("long",False) or "Long Signal" in summary_reason or "ENTERED_long" in summary_reason: status_style="bold bright_green"
        elif signal_check_result.get("short",False) or "Short Signal" in summary_reason or "ENTERED_short" in summary_reason: status_style="bold bright_red"
        elif "Block" in summary_reason or "FAIL:" in summary_reason.upper() or "EmergencyClose" in summary_reason or "Conflict" in summary_reason: status_style="yellow"
        elif "CLOSED_" in summary_reason or "HOLDING_" in summary_reason or "INFO:" in summary_reason: status_style="bright_blue"
        elif not any(s in summary_reason for s in ["No Signal", "Initializing", "Processing..."]): status_style="white"
        
        panel_content.append(Text(f"  Overall: {summary_reason}", style=status_style))

        orig_detail = signal_check_result.get("orig_detail")
        if orig_detail and orig_detail != "N/A":
            orig_wrapped = "\n".join([f"    {line}" for line in textwrap.wrap(f"Orig: {orig_detail}", width=max(20, console.width - 25), subsequent_indent="      ")])
            panel_content.append(Text(f"\n  └─ {orig_wrapped.lstrip()}", style="dim")) # lstrip to remove leading space from wrapped lines for first line

        vt_detail = signal_check_result.get("vt_detail")
        if vt_detail and vt_detail != "N/A":
            vt_wrapped = "\n".join([f"    {line}" for line in textwrap.wrap(f"VT: {vt_detail}", width=max(20, console.width - 25), subsequent_indent="      ")])
            panel_content.append(Text(f"\n  └─ {vt_wrapped.lstrip()}", style="dim"))
        
        console.print(Panel(panel_content, title=f"[bold bright_magenta]{panel_title}[/]", border_style="bright_blue", expand=False, padding=(1,2)))

# --- Trading Bot Class ---
class TradingBot:
    def __init__(self):
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.MAGENTA}--- Initializing Pyrmethus v4.5.8 (Neon Nexus - VT Edition) ---{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        self.config = TradingConfig()
        try:
            self.exchange_manager = ExchangeManager(self.config)
            self.indicator_calculator = IndicatorCalculator(self.config)
            self.signal_generator = SignalGenerator(self.config)
            self.order_manager = OrderManager(self.config, self.exchange_manager)
        except ValueError as ve: # Catch specific init errors from components
            logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}TradingBot component initialization failed: {ve}. Halting.{Style.RESET_ALL}"); sys.exit(1)
        except Exception as e: # Catch any other unexpected errors during setup
            logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}Unexpected critical error during TradingBot component initialization: {e}. Halting.{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True); sys.exit(1)
        
        self.status_display = StatusDisplay(self.config)
        self.shutdown_requested = False
        self._setup_signal_handlers()
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.GREEN}Pyrmethus components initialized. Ready to conjure trades.{Style.RESET_ALL}")

    def _setup_signal_handlers(self):
        signals_to_handle = [signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM]
        for sig in signals_to_handle:
            try: 
                signal.signal(sig, self._signal_handler_callback)
                logger.debug(f"Signal handler for {signal.Signals(sig).name} set up.")
            except (ValueError, OSError, AttributeError, Exception) as e: # More specific exceptions if known
                # This can happen on Windows or other environments not fully supporting POSIX signals
                logger.warning(f"{Fore.YELLOW}Could not set OS signal handler for {signal.Signals(sig).name if hasattr(signal, 'Signals') else sig} (Env Limitation?): {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}")

    def _signal_handler_callback(self, sig_num: int, frame: Optional[Any]): # Frame is unused but required by signal handler signature
        # Get signal name robustly
        sig_name = signal.Signals(sig_num).name if hasattr(signal, 'Signals') and isinstance(sig_num, int) and sig_num in signal.Signals else f"Signal {sig_num}"
        
        if not self.shutdown_requested:
            # Use Rich console for more visible shutdown message if available
            console.print(f"\n[bold yellow]Signal {sig_name} received. Initiating graceful shutdown... Please wait.[/]")
            logger.warning(f"Signal {sig_name} received. Initiating graceful shutdown...")
            self.shutdown_requested = True
        else:
            logger.warning("Shutdown sequence already in progress. Ignoring additional signal.")

    def _display_startup_info(self):
        # log_level_display_name is a global set during logging config
        vt_status = f"VT Strategy Enabled: {Style.BRIGHT}{self.config.vt_enable}{Style.NORMAL}"
        if self.config.vt_enable:
            vt_status += (f" (TrendEMA:{self.config.vt_trend_ema_period}, VWMA:{self.config.vt_vwma_period}, "
                          f"VolLookback:{self.config.vt_volume_spike_lookback}, VolMult:{self.config.vt_volume_spike_multiplier.normalize()})")
        
        startup_text = Text(
            f"Symbol: {self.config.symbol}\nInterval: {self.config.interval}\n"
            f"Market Type: {self.config.market_type} (Category: {self.config.bybit_v5_category})\n"
            f"Position Idx: {self.config.position_idx} (0=OneWay, 1=HedgeBuy, 2=HedgeSell)\n"
            f"Risk/Trade: {self.config.risk_percentage * 100:.3f}%\n"
            f"SL/TP Multipliers (ATR): SL={self.config.sl_atr_multiplier.normalize()}, TP={self.config.tp_atr_multiplier.normalize()}\n"
            f"TSL Activation (ATR Mult): {self.config.tsl_activation_atr_multiplier.normalize()}, TSL Percent: {self.config.trailing_stop_percent.normalize()}%\n"
            f"Trade Only With Trend (Orig Strategy): {Style.BRIGHT}{self.config.trade_only_with_trend}{Style.NORMAL}\n{vt_status}\n"
            f"Journaling: {Style.BRIGHT}{self.config.enable_journaling}{Style.NORMAL} (File: '{self.config.journal_file_path}')\n"
            f"Log Level: {log_level_display_name}", style="bright_white"
        )
        console.print(Panel(startup_text, title="[bold cyan]Pyrmethus Configuration Summary[/]", border_style="cyan", expand=False))

    def run(self):
        self._display_startup_info()
        termux_notify(f"Pyrmethus Started", f"Trading {self.config.symbol} on {self.config.interval}.")
        cycle_count = 0
        while not self.shutdown_requested:
            cycle_count += 1
            cycle_start_monotonic = time.monotonic()
            logger.debug(f"{Fore.BLUE}--- Trading Cycle {cycle_count} Start ---{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            try: 
                self.trading_spell_cycle(cycle_count)
            except KeyboardInterrupt: 
                logger.warning("\nKeyboardInterrupt in main loop. Initiating shutdown."); self.shutdown_requested = True; break
            except ccxt.AuthenticationError as auth_err: 
                logger.critical(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}CRITICAL AUTH ERROR (Cycle {cycle_count}): {auth_err}. Halting.{Style.RESET_ALL}",exc_info=False)
                termux_notify("Pyrmethus CRITICAL ERROR",f"Auth failed: {str(auth_err)[:100]}"); self.shutdown_requested=True; break
            except SystemExit as se: 
                logger.warning(f"SystemExit (code {se.code}) in trading cycle. Terminating."); self.shutdown_requested=True; break
            except Exception as cycle_err:
                logger.error(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}Unhandled exception in trading cycle {cycle_count}: {cycle_err}{Style.RESET_ALL}", exc_info=True)
                termux_notify("Pyrmethus Cycle Error", f"Exception in cycle {cycle_count}. Check logs.")
                # Longer sleep after unhandled error in a cycle to prevent rapid error loops
                sleep_after_err = self.config.loop_sleep_seconds * 2 
                logger.info(f"Sleeping for {sleep_after_err}s after cycle error before retry."); time.sleep(sleep_after_err); continue
            
            cycle_duration = time.monotonic() - cycle_start_monotonic
            logger.debug(f"Cycle {cycle_count} completed in {cycle_duration:.2f}s.")
            
            if not self.shutdown_requested:
                sleep_needed = max(0, self.config.loop_sleep_seconds - cycle_duration)
                if sleep_needed > 0:
                    logger.debug(f"Sleeping for {sleep_needed:.2f}s until next cycle...")
                    # Sleep in small chunks to allow faster shutdown response
                    sleep_end_time = time.monotonic() + sleep_needed
                    try:
                        while time.monotonic() < sleep_end_time and not self.shutdown_requested:
                            time.sleep(min(0.5, sleep_needed)) # Sleep for 0.5s or remaining time
                    except KeyboardInterrupt: 
                        logger.warning("\nKeyboardInterrupt during sleep. Initiating shutdown."); self.shutdown_requested = True
            
            if self.shutdown_requested: logger.info("Shutdown requested. Exiting main trading loop."); break
        
        self.graceful_shutdown()
        console.print(f"\n[bold bright_cyan]Pyrmethus ({self.config.symbol}) has completed its session and returned to the ether.[/]")

    def trading_spell_cycle(self, cycle_num: int):
        # Initialize status dict for this cycle for the display panel
        current_status_dict: Dict[str, Any] = {"summary": f"Cycle {cycle_num} Processing...", "orig_detail": "N/A", "vt_detail": "N/A"}
        
        ohlcv_df = self.exchange_manager.fetch_ohlcv()
        if ohlcv_df is None or ohlcv_df.empty:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Cycle {cycle_num} Aborted: Failed to fetch OHLCV data.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"FAIL_CYCLE_{cycle_num}:FETCH_OHLCV"})
            self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, None, None, None, None, None, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info); return

        try: # Extract latest price and timestamp
            latest_candle = ohlcv_df.iloc[-1]
            current_price = safe_decimal(latest_candle["close"])
            last_candle_ts = ohlcv_df.index[-1].to_pydatetime() # Convert Pandas Timestamp to datetime
            if current_price.is_nan() or current_price <= Decimal("0"): 
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid latest close price: {current_price.normalize() if not current_price.is_nan() else 'NaN'}")
            logger.debug(f"Latest Candle: Ts={last_candle_ts.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z')}, Price={current_price.normalize()}")
        except (IndexError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError) as e:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Cycle {cycle_num} Aborted: Error processing latest candle: {e}{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"FAIL_CYCLE_{cycle_num}:PROCESS_CANDLE ({type(e).__name__})"})
            self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, None, None, None, None, None, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info); return

        indicators = self.indicator_calculator.calculate_indicators(ohlcv_df)
        if not indicators: # Includes critical ATR NaN case handled in calculator
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Cycle {cycle_num} Aborted: Failed to calculate indicators.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"FAIL_CYCLE_{cycle_num}:CALC_INDICATORS"})
            self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, last_candle_ts, current_price, None, None, None, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info); return
        
        indicators["close_price"] = current_price # Add current price for convenience in signal/exit checks

        total_equity, _ = self.exchange_manager.get_balance()
        if total_equity is None or total_equity.is_nan() or total_equity <= Decimal("0"):
            equity_val_str = str(total_equity.normalize() if total_equity and not total_equity.is_nan() else total_equity)
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Cycle {cycle_num} Aborted: Invalid total equity ({equity_val_str}) or zero/negative.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"FAIL_CYCLE_{cycle_num}:FETCH_EQUITY_INVALID"})
            self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, last_candle_ts, current_price, indicators, None, total_equity, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info); return

        current_positions = self.exchange_manager.get_current_position()
        if current_positions is None:
            logger.error(f"{Fore.RED}Cycle {cycle_num} Aborted: Failed to fetch current position.{Style.RESET_ALL}")
            current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"FAIL_CYCLE_{cycle_num}:FETCH_POSITION"})
            self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, last_candle_ts, current_price, indicators, None, total_equity, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info); return

        active_pos_side: Optional[str] = None; active_pos_details: Optional[Dict[str,Any]] = None
        if current_positions.get("long",{}): active_pos_side, active_pos_details = "long", current_positions["long"]
        elif current_positions.get("short",{}): active_pos_side, active_pos_details = "short", current_positions["short"]

        if active_pos_side and active_pos_details: # Currently in a position
            pos_qty = safe_decimal(active_pos_details.get("qty"))
            pos_entry_px = safe_decimal(active_pos_details.get("entry_price"))
            current_atr = indicators.get("atr", Decimal("NaN")) # type: ignore
            
            # Manage TSL if applicable (only if SL/TP is active, not if TSL already active)
            if (self.order_manager.protection_tracker.get(active_pos_side) == "ACTIVE_SLTP" and 
                not any(v.is_nan() or v <= Decimal("0") for v in [pos_entry_px, current_price, current_atr])): # type: ignore
                self.order_manager.manage_trailing_stop(active_pos_side, pos_entry_px, current_price, current_atr) # type: ignore
                # If TSL was activated, re-fetch position to reflect new protection status for display
                if self.order_manager.protection_tracker.get(active_pos_side) == "ACTIVE_TSL":
                    logger.debug("Re-fetching position summary after TSL management for display update."); 
                    current_positions = self.exchange_manager.get_current_position() # Update positions
                    if current_positions: active_pos_details = current_positions.get(active_pos_side, {}) # Update details
                    else: active_pos_details = None # Failed to fetch, clear details
            
            # Check for strategy-based exit signals if TSL is not yet managing the exit
            if self.order_manager.protection_tracker.get(active_pos_side) != "ACTIVE_TSL":
                exit_reason = self.signal_generator.check_exit_signals(active_pos_side, indicators)
                if exit_reason:
                    logger.trade(f"Attempting to close {active_pos_side.upper()} position due to: {exit_reason}")
                    if not pos_qty.is_nan() and pos_qty > Decimal("0"): # type: ignore
                        close_ok = self.order_manager.close_position(active_pos_side, pos_qty, reason=exit_reason) # type: ignore
                        current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"CLOSED_{active_pos_side.upper()}_SIGNAL: {exit_reason.split(':')[0]}" if close_ok else f"FAIL:CLOSE_SIGNAL_{active_pos_side.upper()}"}) # Shorten reason for summary
                        current_positions = self.exchange_manager.get_current_position() # Update after close attempt
                        self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, last_candle_ts, current_price, indicators, current_positions, total_equity, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info); return
                    else: logger.warning(f"Exit signal for {active_pos_side.upper()} but pos qty invalid ({pos_qty}). Cannot close.")
            
            # Re-check position state after all actions (TSL, exit signals) to see if it was closed by exchange stops
            current_positions_after_actions = self.exchange_manager.get_current_position()
            if current_positions_after_actions is None: 
                logger.warning(f"Failed to re-fetch position state for {active_pos_side} after TSL/exit checks. Status may be stale.")
            else:
                current_positions = current_positions_after_actions # Update main variable
                # Determine if still in position
                new_long_pos, new_short_pos = current_positions.get("long",{}), current_positions.get("short",{})
                if new_long_pos and safe_decimal(new_long_pos.get("qty","0")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON: active_pos_side, active_pos_details = "long", new_long_pos
                elif new_short_pos and safe_decimal(new_short_pos.get("qty","0")).copy_abs() >= POSITION_QTY_EPSILON: active_pos_side, active_pos_details = "short", new_short_pos
                else: # Position is now flat
                    if active_pos_side: # Implies it was closed during this cycle by exchange stop or other means
                        logger.info(f"Position {active_pos_side.upper()} appears closed by exchange stop or earlier action during cycle.");
                        current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"INFO:POS_{active_pos_side.upper()}_CLOSED_EXCH_OR_SIGNAL"})
                        self.order_manager.protection_tracker[active_pos_side.lower()] = None # Reset tracker
                    active_pos_side, active_pos_details = None, None # Now flat

        if not active_pos_side: # Flat, check for new entry signals
            logger.debug("Currently flat. Checking for new entry signals...")
            entry_signals_result = self.signal_generator.generate_signals(ohlcv_df, indicators)
            current_status_dict = entry_signals_result # Update status with signal generation outcome
            
            entry_order_side: Optional[str] = "buy" if entry_signals_result.get("long") else "sell" if entry_signals_result.get("short") else None
            if entry_order_side:
                current_atr = indicators.get("atr", Decimal("NaN")) # type: ignore
                if not current_atr.is_nan() and current_atr > Decimal("0"): # type: ignore
                    entry_ok = self.order_manager.place_risked_market_order(entry_order_side, current_atr, total_equity, current_price) # type: ignore
                    entered_side_logical = "long" if entry_order_side == "buy" else "short"
                    # Update status based on entry attempt outcome for the panel
                    current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"ENTERED_{entered_side_logical.upper()}" if entry_ok else f"FAIL:ENTRY_{entered_side_logical.upper()}"})
                    current_positions = self.exchange_manager.get_current_position() # Refresh position state
                    self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, last_candle_ts, current_price, indicators, current_positions, total_equity, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info); return
                else: 
                    logger.warning(f"Cannot attempt {entry_order_side.upper()} entry: Missing/invalid ATR ({current_atr}).")
                    current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"FAIL:ENTRY_DATA_MISSING_ATR_{entry_order_side.upper()}"})
        else: # Still in position, update status for display
            current_status_dict.update({"summary": f"HOLDING_{active_pos_side.upper()}"})
        
        # Default display if no early return from entry/exit actions
        self.status_display.print_status_panel(cycle_num, last_candle_ts, current_price, indicators, current_positions, total_equity, current_status_dict, self.order_manager.protection_tracker, self.exchange_manager.market_info)

    def graceful_shutdown(self):
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.YELLOW}--- Pyrmethus Graceful Shutdown Initiated ---{Style.RESET_ALL}")
        # Note: Positions with exchange-side protection (SL/TP/TSL) will remain active.
        # No specific cleanup (like closing all positions) is configured by default here.
        # If you need to close all positions on shutdown, add logic here.
        logger.info("Graceful shutdown: Exchange-managed protections (SL/TP/TSL) on open positions will remain. No other specific cleanup actions configured.")
        termux_notify("Pyrmethus Shutdown", f"Bot for {self.config.symbol} is shutting down.")
        logger.info(f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.YELLOW}--- Pyrmethus Shutdown Complete ---{Style.RESET_ALL}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        bot = TradingBot()
        bot.run()
    except SystemExit as e: # Catch sys.exit() calls, e.g. from config validation
        if e.code == 0: logger.info("Pyrmethus terminated normally.")
        else: logger.warning(f"Pyrmethus terminated with exit code: {e.code}")
        sys.exit(e.code) # Propagate the exit code
    except Exception as main_exception:
        # Use logger if available, otherwise print
        log_func = logger.critical if 'logger' in globals() and hasattr(logger, 'critical') else print
        
        err_msg_prefix = "CRITICAL UNHANDLED EXCEPTION in Pyrmethus main execution:"
        # Check if colorama Fore/Style are functional (not dummy classes)
        is_colorama_functional = _COLORAMA_SUCCESSFULLY_IMPORTED 
        
        if is_colorama_functional:
            err_msg = f"{Style.BRIGHT}{Fore.RED}{err_msg_prefix} {main_exception}{Style.RESET_ALL}"
        else:
            err_msg = f"{err_msg_prefix} {main_exception}"
        
        log_func(err_msg, exc_info=True) # Log with full traceback

        if 'termux_notify' in globals() and callable(termux_notify): # Check if termux_notify is defined
            termux_notify("Pyrmethus CRASHED", "Critical unhandled exception. Check logs!")
        sys.exit(1) # Exit with error code
```
